<h3 id="156">[156] Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28860](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jeanmely Rojas Nunez, Viraj Sawant, Nathan Allen, Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar, Yannis Zongo, Vasu Sharma, Maheep Chaudhary
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Mechanistic Interpretability, Fine-Tuning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability)
- **Summary**: 问题：微调LLMs会导致灾难性遗忘，RL比SFT更好地保留先前能力。方法：引入微分电路脆弱性，在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct上比较RL和SFT。结果：SFT适应更快但电路破坏更大，RL保留更多电路但适应较慢。

<h3 id="157">[157] Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28863](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aalok Patwa
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Game AI, Reinforcement Learning, Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：不完美信息多人游戏（Big 2）中智能体的学习挑战。方法：开发自对弈RL框架，比较PPO、MC Q、SARSA和Q-learning。结果：PPO优于其他方法；适度熵正则化有助于；当前策略自对弈提供最佳课程。

<h3 id="158">[158] Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28865](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiayi Fang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model-Based RL, Embodied AI, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：世界模型在没有语言监督的物理探索中学到什么？方法：训练基于VAE的世界模型进行随机具身探索，分析潜在空间结构。结果：方向准确率0.677 vs 0.547随机，位置RSA 0.192 vs 0.029随机（6.6倍提升）；预测性能和语义对齐共同改进。

<h3 id="159">[159] Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28866](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Musheng Li, Ziying Zhang, Cheng jin, Yuantao Gu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Analysis, Tokenization
- **Summary**: 问题：基于令牌的时间序列LLMs忽略了时间序列令牌的连续性和序数性。方法：提出COM，一种连续性和序数性感知策略，在初始化和训练中加入几何约束。结果：在多个时间序列分析基准上持续提高了令牌基TS-LLMs的性能。

<h3 id="160">[160] PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28867](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junru Zhang, Lang Feng, Jinbo Wang, Xu Guo, Yucheng Wang, Han Yu, Min Wu, Yabo Dong, Duanqing Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Generation, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 问题：流匹配在时间序列生成中因全局向量场估计器导致频谱失真和模式覆盖差。方法：提出PrismFlow，采用Koopman启发动力专家和置信度感知胜者全吃目标。结果：在Context-FID上提升15.6%，判别分数提升38.6%，在低数据设置下鲁棒。

<h3 id="161">[161] TaxDistill: Improving Metagenomic Taxonomic Annotation via Distilled Genomic Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28868](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28868) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rongye Ye, Lun Li, Zheng Luo, Yiran Zhan, Shuhui Song
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Genomic AI
- **Summary**: 针对宏基因组分类中相似性搜索标签带来的噪声问题，提出TaxDistill知识蒸馏框架，以500M参数的基因组基础模型GenomeOcean作为教师网络生成软标签，蒸馏到轻量学生网络。在CAMI2数据集上显著提升F1分数（如胃肠道数据集从0.763到0.941）。

<h3 id="162">[162] Balancing Multimodal Learning through Label Space Reshaping</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28869](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28869) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaoyu Ma, Weijie Zhang, Yuanhao Gao, Han Miao, Yongjian Deng, Hao Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Multimodal Alignment
- **Summary**: 针对多模态学习中模态不平衡问题，提出平衡多模态标签重塑（BMLR）方法，通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡各模态的映射难度。实验表明BMLR能持续提升多模态性能。

<h3 id="163">[163] Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28870](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kiril Bangachev, Guy Bresler, Yury Polyanskiy
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Sparse Autoencoder
- **Summary**: 研究柏拉图表示假说（PRH），提出信号-偏差-噪声三部分框架，发现表示对齐源于对象-属性关系的线性编码。通过稀疏自编码器提取线性特征，证明稀疏表示对齐更强，并分析了偏差和噪声的影响。

<h3 id="164">[164] LogDx-CI: Benchmarking Log Reduction Tools for LLM Root-Cause Diagnosis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28876](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28876) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bowen Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Software Testing, Debugging
- **Summary**: 针对CI失败日志庞大嘈杂，缺乏日志缩减工具比较的问题，提出LogDx-CI基准，比较11种工具在35个真实案例上的效果。发现混合grep+tail路由器在成本和质量上占优，且代理循环可缩小质量差距但成本差异仍存。

<h3 id="165">[165] GrowLoop: Self-Evolving Conversation Evaluation Seeded by Human</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28882](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28882) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yihang Lin, Yunze Gao, Zeyang Lin, Dongbo Li, Kun Peng, Chenglong Song, Yue Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Dialogue System, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对开放式对话中人类相似性评估的隐性知识、评判差异和进化标准，提出GrowLoop自进化评估系统。通过人类种子和LLM代理迭代提取评估准则，实现准则-案例协同进化。生成的基准能有效区分模型能力并适应新场景。

<h3 id="166">[166] Context Distillation as Latent Memory Management</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28889](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28889) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziyang Zheng, Zeju Li, Xiangyu Wen, Jianyuan Zhong, Junhua Huang, Lei Chen, Mingxuan Yuan, Qiang Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 针对上下文蒸馏中多个潜记忆的存储、检索和安全激活问题，提出将上下文蒸馏视为潜记忆管理。每个上下文蒸馏为独立的LoRA适配器，形成记忆库，通过检索、路由和自门控机制决定是否激活。实验表明优于基线，自门控提升鲁棒性。

<h3 id="167">[167] Quantum-Enhanced Adversarial Robustness in Artificial Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28899](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28899) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jaydip Sen
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Quantum Computing
- **Venue**: Quantum Enhancements to the AI Industry
- **Summary**: 综述量子增强的对抗鲁棒性，首先介绍对抗机器学习及其防御策略，接着引入量子计算和量子机器学习模型，最后提出量子增强鲁棒性的概念框架和应用挑战。

<h3 id="168">[168] Hallucination Detection-Guided Preference Optimization for Clinical Summarization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28910](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28910) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shamanth Kuthpadi Seethakantha, Dung Ngoc Thai, Vara Prasad Gudi, Simran Tiwari, Rami Matar, Avijit Mitra, Wenlong Zhao, Wael Salloum, Andrew McCallum
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Summarization, Preference Optimization, Clinical NLP
- **Summary**: 针对临床摘要生成中的幻觉问题，提出基于幻觉检测的偏好优化方法。先利用幻觉检测器指导迭代修正，再将修正轨迹转化为偏好对微调模型。在MIMIC-IV临床笔记上使Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct幻觉减少48%。

<h3 id="169">[169] AIRGuard: Guarding Agent Actions with Runtime Authority Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28914](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28914) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Suliu Qin, Haomin Zhuang, Yujun Zhou, Yufei Han, Xiangliang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Security
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Sophie508/AIRGuard)
- **Summary**: 针对工具使用语言代理的“权限混淆”攻击，提出运行时护卫AIRGuard，通过操作权限最小化策略，在动作执行前进行权限验证。在AgentTrap上将攻击成功率从36.3%降至5.5%，并保持良好良性效用。

<h3 id="170">[170] First head-to-head comparison of agentic AI applied to the analysis of simulated data of the Einstein Telescope</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28916](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28916) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gianluca Inguglia
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.IM; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Scientific Reasoning
- **Summary**: 首次头对头比较两种自主AI系统（Claude Code和Codex）执行引力波数据分析管线。两者均完成科学任务，但行为成本不同：Claude Code更快但有静默偏差，Codex较慢但显式纠错。科学结果一致，但生成手稿质量有差异。

<h3 id="171">[171] CosmicFish-HRM: Adaptive Reasoning via Hierarchical Recurrent Mechanisms in Compact Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28919](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28919) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Venkat Akhil Lakkapragada
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Adaptive Computation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MistyozAI/CosmicFish-HRM)
- **Summary**: 探索紧凑语言模型中的自适应推理深度，提出CosmicFish-HRM，内含层次推理模块动态分配计算资源。模型学习到非均匀推理步数，表明自适应深度可替代参数规模提升推理能力。

<h3 id="172">[172] Conf-Gen: Conformal Uncertainty Quantification for Generative Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28920](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28920) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem, Kevin Zhang, Wei Cui, Marc T. Law, Kin Kwan Leung
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Conformal Prediction
- **Summary**: 针对无监督生成模型无法直接应用共形预测的问题，提出共形生成（Conf-Gen）框架，将共形风险控制适应性扩展到生成任务。在图像生成、对话系统等上展示了共形保证的可行性。

<h3 id="173">[173] Beyond Recall: Behavioral Specification as an Interpretive Layer for AI Personalization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28969](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28969) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aarik Gulaya
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/agulaya24/beyond-recall)
- **Summary**: 针对AI个性化中用户对齐问题，提出行为规范作为解释层，以压缩用户数据为模式上下文。在基准上提升表示准确性并降低对冲，以25倍压缩成本实现大部分原始语料效果。

<h3 id="174">[174] Comparing Post-Hoc Explainable AI Methods for Interpreting Black-Box EEG Models in Depression Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28977](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28977) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Antonia Šarčević, Nikolina Frid
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Medical AI, EEG Analysis
- **Summary**: 比较多种事后可解释方法在抑郁症EEG检测模型上的应用。发现不同方法在前额、颞叶等区域有部分一致归因，但存在变异性。强调了可解释性在精神科应用中的价值和局限。

<h3 id="175">[175] The Hamilton-Jacobi Theory of Deep Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28983](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28983) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jose Marie Antonio Miñoza, Erika Fille T. Legara, Christopher P. Monterola
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.DS; math.RT
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Neural Architecture
- **Summary**: 将神经网络训练精确识别为哈密顿-雅可比初值问题的搜索，对log-sum-exp层精确对应，对ResNet、Transformer等结构成立。推导了泛化速率、对抗鲁棒性等定量结果。

<h3 id="176">[176] Measuring Real-World Prompt Injection Attacks in LLM-based Resume Screening</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28999](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28999) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohan Zhang, Yuqi Jia, Zhen Tan, Steven Jiang, Neil Zhenqiang Gong, Tianlong Chen, Dawn Song
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Prompt Injection
- **Venue**: USENIX Security Symposium 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/UNITES-Lab/resume-injection-measurement)
- **Summary**: 首次系统研究LLM简历筛选中的真实提示注入攻击。基于约20万份简历检测发现约1%含有隐藏注入，且近年来明显增加。大多数注入不使用显式指令。

<h3 id="177">[177] FormInv: A Measurement Protocol for Semantic Invariance in Mathematical Reasoning Benchmarks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29001](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29001) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nishal Thomas, Noel Thomas
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 该论文指出数学推理基准中存在的语义不一致问题，例如MathCheck基准中3.1%的语义错误会导致模型排名变化。提出的FormInv协议通过跨模型投票自动检测语义错误，并引入语义一致性率（SCR）作为评估指标。实验表明，标准基准无法捕捉模型在语义等价表述下的表现差距，而FormInv能有效揭示排名变化并提供模型选择指导。

<h3 id="178">[178] LoRe: Adaptive Interaction-Evaluation Routing with Per-Step Interaction Budgets for Iterative Graph Solvers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29005](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29005) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jintao Li, Yong-Yi Wang, Zheng-An Wang, Heng Fan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Combinatorial Optimization, Inference Optimization
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 现有图神经网络求解器在每次迭代中评估所有边/因子交互，导致计算和内存开销大。本文提出LoRe，一种无需训练的即插即用方法，通过动态路由仅评估高冲突或高不确定性的交互，从而大幅降低计算和内存消耗。在最大独立集和旅行商问题等任务上，LoRe实现了8-15倍加速和12-44倍内存减少，同时保持解质量。

<h3 id="179">[179] Label-Free Reinforcement Learning via Cross-Model Entropy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29009](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29009) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Matt Gorbett, Hossein Shirazi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Reward Model, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 现有强化学习训练大语言模型依赖于可验证奖励或人工标注，成本高且易受奖励破解。本文提出跨模型熵（CME），利用独立验证器模型对生成响应的平均对数似然作为无标签奖励信号，避免自我强化错误。在开放指令遵循任务上，CME结合GRPO在多个模型家族中显著提升了胜率（52.5%-71.4%）。

<h3 id="180">[180] Return-to-Go Is More Than a Number: Q-Guided Alignment for Return-Conditioned Supervised Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29028](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29028) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxiao Yang, Weitong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Offline RL, Reward Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 现有条件序列模型（CSM）将返回策略（RTG）作为数值输入，但未与策略性能对齐。本文提出Q-ALIGN DT框架，通过Q函数确保输出策略的Q值与输入RTG一致，并使用RTG扰动技术微调Q函数。理论证明该方法能学习近最优策略，实验在D4RL基准上展示了优越的可控性和性能。

<h3 id="181">[181] SCDBench: A Benchmark for LLM-Based Smart Contract Decompilers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29059](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29059) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaihua Qin, Dawn Song, Arthur Gervais
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Benchmark, LLM Evaluation, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: 智能合约反编译评估缺乏统一数据集和标准，现有研究指标不一致。本文提出SCDBench，包含600个真实Solidity合约的数据集和四阶段评估方法（格式完整性、可编译性、ABI恢复、语义一致性）。实验表明前沿LLM能生成结构化代码，但语义一致性仍远未解决，最佳模型仅完美反编译42/600合约。

<h3 id="182">[182] Structured Prompt Optimization Meets Reinforcement Learning for Global and Local Interpretability over Complex Text</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29076](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29076) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianyang Zhou, Wenbo Chen, Pierre Jinghong Liang, Leman Akoglu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Prompt Engineering, Interpretability, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 文本分类的监督微调缺乏推理透明性，离散提示优化性能不足。本文提出eXTC，通过结构化提示优化学习自然语言规则书（SOP），然后将SOP推理蒸馏到小型LM，并利用强化学习扩展推理能力。该方法在分类性能和解释质量上显著优于现有范式，并提供全局可解释模块和局部推理轨迹。

<h3 id="183">[183] Same Question, Different Source, Different Answer: Auditing Source-Dependence in Medical Multi-Source RAG</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29084](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29084) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yubo Li, Rema Padman, Ramayya Krishnan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Evaluation, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 多源RAG系统可能因检索来源不同而给出矛盾答案，但现有评估范式无法诊断。本文提出源依赖性审计框架，发布TransplantQA基准、HERO-QA检索策略和结构化输出裁判，用于评估跨来源关系。实验显示更好的检索揭示了更多分歧，且该框架可迁移至法律和教育领域。

<h3 id="184">[184] OISD: On-Policy Internal Self-Distillation of Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29089](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29089) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Liu, Darryl Cherian Jacob, Yang Zhou, Jindong Wang, Pan He
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/THE-MALT-LAB/OISD)
- **Summary**: 现有RL后训练主要优化最终输出策略，忽视中间表示中的预测信号。本文提出OISD框架，通过在策略分布上利用最终层作为内部教师，引导中间层对齐（对数对齐和注意力对齐），并结合GRPO进行优化。在四个数学推理任务上，OISD显著提升了推理性能。

<h3 id="185">[185] GEO-Bench: Benchmarking Ranking Manipulation in Generative Engine Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29107](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29107) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ojas Nimase, Zhe Chen, Gengpei Qi, Yue Zhao, Xiyang Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, LLM Security, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 生成式引擎优化（GEO）的排名操纵方法缺乏统一比较。本文提出GEO-Bench，在固定开放权重排位器上统一评估黑盒、白盒攻击和白帽策略，使用有效性（NRG等）和隐蔽性（违规率等）指标。实验显示攻击效果与隐蔽性存在权衡，黑盒重写方法在某些领域能规避检测。

<h3 id="186">[186] unix-ctf: Procedural Environments for Unix-Competence Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29115](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29115) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Geoffrey Bradway, Roger Creus Castanyer, Lorenz Wolf, Maxwill Lin, Matthew James Sargent, Augustine N. Mavor-Parker
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent, CLI Agent
- **Summary**: 当前终端基准无法区分Unix能力和编程能力。本文提出unix-ctf，一个基于LLM辅助生成Unix技能任务的流程，包含656个可复用变体对应155种技术。使用GRPO微调Qwen3-8B将解决率从11.6%提升至43.6%，表明Unix能力是可分离可训练的。

<h3 id="187">[187] A Minimal Bifurcation Model of Load Imbalance in a Softmax Mixture-of-Experts Router</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29121](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29121) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: O. M. Kiselev
- **Subjects**: math.DS; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Training Dynamics
- **Summary**: 混合专家模型（MoE）的负载不均衡现象缺乏机理理解。本文提出一个双专家softmax路由的最小动力系统模型，通过平均场极限推导出平衡态的分岔行为：弱反馈时仅有稳定平衡，强反馈时出现不对称态。数值实验验证了该模型与真实MoE训练中负载不均衡的关联。

<h3 id="188">[188] When and How Long? The Readout-Mediator Angle in Temporal Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29126](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29126) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shreyas Fadnavis, Praitayini Kanakaraj, Felix Wyss
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, LLM Reasoning, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 线性探针能高精度解码表示，但可能与模型实际计算无关。本文在日期推理任务中发现，探针方向与因果子空间正交，探针效果可被轻易消除。通过电路分析揭示注意力头和MLP的分工，探针特征与因果特征语义不重叠。该现象在多模型和多任务中复现，质疑了探针作为安全监控的可靠性。

<h3 id="189">[189] Multi-Resolution End-to-End Deep Neural Network for Optimizing Latency-Accuracy Tradeoff in Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29138](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29138) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qitao Weng, Heechul Yun
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Computer Vision
- **Summary**: 自动驾驶中固定分辨率模型在动态场景下无法最优平衡延迟和精度。本文提出多分辨率端到端CNN，支持运行时选择输入分辨率和分辨率重定向。在CARLA模拟中，该方法在车道入侵、红绿灯违规和碰撞等安全指标上持续优于固定分辨率基线。

<h3 id="190">[190] Toward User Preference Alignment in LLM Recommendation via Explicit Context Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29141](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29141) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weizhi Zhang, Wooseong Yang, Yuxin Cui, Zhaohui Guo, Hins Hu, Liangwei Yang, Henry Peng Zou, Qifei Wang, Hanqing Zeng, Jiayi Liu, Yinglong Xia, Philip S. Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, LLM Personalization, User Modeling
- **Venue**: CogMI 2025
- **Summary**: 传统推荐系统依赖隐式信号，忽略用户显式文本反馈（如评论），导致偏好错位和过滤气泡。本文倡导在LLM推荐中优先利用显式上下文反馈，回顾推荐范式演变，提出利用用户生成内容进行偏好对齐的框架，以实现更个性化和可解释的推荐。

<h3 id="191">[191] SafeRx-Agent: A Knowledge-Grounded Multi-Agent Framework for Safe and Explainable Medication Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29146](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29146) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Wang, Hanwei Wu, Zhenghan Tai, Sicheng Lyu, Qincheng Lu, Ziyu Zhao, Jijun Chi, Jingrui Tian, Xiao-Wen Chang, Ziyang Song
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multi-Agent System, Recommender System
- **Summary**: 现有药物推荐方法要么只预测结构化代码缺乏可解释性，要么缺乏安全验证。本文提出细粒度药物推荐设置，基于四位ATC编码，并构建SafeRx-Agent多智能体框架，利用患者上下文、外部知识和安全验证生成可追溯的药物集合。在MIMIC数据集上提高了预测精度并控制了药物相互作用。

<h3 id="192">[192] Real-rootedness of the Poincaré polynomials of $\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}$: an AI-assisted proof</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29151](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29151) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gergely Bérczi, Young-Hoon Kiem
- **Subjects**: math.AG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文证明了关于模空间Poincaré多项式的Aluffi-Chen-Marcolli猜想，即其实根性和超对数凹性。证明通过双变量变形和Sturm-Rolle论证实现，并推广到Fulton-MacPherson空间。该证明由AI辅助完成，人类负责提出问题、评估和组合最终可验证证明。

<h3 id="193">[193] Unveiling Multi-regime Patterns in SciML: Distinct Failure Modes and Regime-specific Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29153](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29153) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxin Wang, Yuanzhe Hu, Xiaokun Zhong, Xiaopeng Wang, Haiquan Lu, Tianyu Pang, Michael W. Mahoney, Yujun Yan, Pu Ren, Yaoqing Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Scientific Computing, Training Dynamics
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对科学机器学习（SciML）模型在不同超参数下表现出多区域行为的问题，提出了一种区域感知的诊断框架，联合分析性能、训练动态和损失景观几何。研究发现三类标准SciML模型（PINNs、神经算子、神经常微分方程）存在一致的三区域结构，且优化效果具有区域特异性。实验验证了该框架在多种SciML基准上的有效性，揭示了传统损失景观指标可能无法捕捉的细粒度失效模式。

<h3 id="194">[194] CA-AC-MPC: CUDA-Accelerated Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29155](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29155) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Antoonio Buo, Vittorio Cammarota, Michele Avagnale, Pierluigi Arpenti, Vincenzo Lippiello, Fabio Ruggiero
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Model Predictive Control, Reinforcement Learning, Robotics
- **Venue**: ICUAS 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对演员-评论家模型预测控制（AC-MPC）中可微MPC层重复求解优化问题导致的高延迟，提出了CUDA加速版本。在敏捷无人机竞速任务上，该方法在保持控制性能的同时显著减少了训练和推理时间，实现了最先进的圈速和接近极限的动态行为。

<h3 id="195">[195] Parallax: Parameterized Local Linear Attention for Language Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29157](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29157) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yifei Zuo, Dhruv Pai, Zhichen Zeng, Alec Dewulf, Shuming Hu, Zhaoran Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, LLM Training, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 该论文针对局部线性注意力（LLA）在大语言模型预训练中因计算和数值稳定性问题无法扩展，提出了Parallax——一种参数化的局部线性注意力机制。Parallax消除了数值求解器，学习额外的查询式投影器，并设计了硬件感知算法。在0.6B和1.7B规模上，Parallax持续降低了困惑度，解码核在多种配置下匹配或超越FlashAttention 2/3，且Muon优化器进一步释放了其潜力。

<h3 id="196">[196] Evolutionary Refinement of Generative Graph Topologies: A Hybrid WGAN-GA Approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29161](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29161) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: James Sargant, Seyedeh Ava Razi Razavi, Renata Dividino, Sheridan Houghten
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Generative Model, Evolutionary Computation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对基于GAN的图生成方法在度和谱分布等结构属性上存在偏差，提出了一种混合WGAN-GA方法，用遗传算法优化生成图的边。实验结果表明，GA优化持续降低了组合最大均值差异（MMD），使生成图更接近真实图的结构模式，同时保持了多样性和新颖性。

<h3 id="197">[197] Domain-Informed Representation for Evolutionary Sieving in Integral and Module Lattices</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29169](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29169) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ahmad Tashfeen, Qi Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Evolutionary Computation, Quantum Computing, Lattice-Based Cryptography
- **Venue**: EvoApplications 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对传统密码学易受量子计算攻击，增强Laarhoven的遗传算法用于最短向量问题（SVP），通过引入领域相关的SVP表示和交叉操作，并扩展到模格。该方法发表在EvoApplications 2026上，为后量子密码学提供了新思路。

<h3 id="198">[198] UA-Legal-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Models on Ukrainian Legal Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29170](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29170) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Volodymyr Ovcharov
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Legal AI, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对法律NLP基准主要集中在英语，缺乏对形态丰富非拉丁语系的评估，提出了UA-Legal-Bench——一个包含五个任务的乌克兰语法律推理基准。在11个LLM上的评估显示，少样本提示在判决形式分类上提升高达38.6个百分点，但在结果预测上效果不一；准确率在不平衡任务中具有误导性，最佳模型的宏F1仅为44%。

<h3 id="199">[199] Sustainable Metal-Organic Framework Water Harvesters in the Artificial Intelligence Era</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29179](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29179) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Reid A. Coyle, Shyam Chand Pal, Peter Walther, Saeun Park, Bin Feng, Zhiling Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Materials Science, LLM Application, Material Discovery
- **Summary**: 该论文综述了金属有机框架（MOF）在大气集水中的应用，并探讨了人工智能（包括大语言模型和数据挖掘）如何加速高性能吸附剂的发现。文章强调了MOF设计原则和AI在预测合成、逆向设计及建立结构-性能关系中的作用，为下一代MOF集水器提供了方向。

<h3 id="200">[200] TIMEGATE: Sustainable Time-Boxed Promotion Gates for Continual ML Adaptation Under Resource Constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29183) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abhijit Chakrabroty, Suddhasvatta Das, Kevin A. Gary, Yash Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Resource Allocation, Training Efficiency
- **Summary**: 该论文针对持续机器学习中每次训练循环消耗大量计算、标注和能源，提出了TIMEGATE——一种通过预算时间、标注、训练和评估来管理持续适应的策略层。在Adult表格数据上，标注比训练高效2.3倍；在LLaMA-3.1-8B上转移到SST-2任务，准确率从0.80提升至0.96；100轮模拟节省66%的评估计算，无静默误提升；在H200上节省89%的时钟时间和能耗。

<h3 id="201">[201] Influence-Guided Symbolic Regression: Scientific Discovery via LLM-Driven Equation Search with Granular Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29184](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29184) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Evgeny S. Saveliev, Samuel Holt, Nabeel Seedat, David L. Bentley, Jim Weatherall, Mihaela van der Schaar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Symbolic Regression, Monte Carlo Tree Search, Scientific Discovery
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有基于LLM的符号回归使用全局MSE等粗糙反馈，无法识别驱动方程性能的组件，提出了IGSR方法，结合细粒度影响分数和蒙特卡洛树搜索。在多种基准上有效，并在真实生物学数据中发现DNA甲基化与RNA聚合酶II暂停之间的新关系，经湿实验验证。

<h3 id="202">[202] Stochastic Lifting for Generating Trajectories of Stochastic Physical Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29194](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29194) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jules Berman, Tobias Blickhan, Benjamin Peherstorfer
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.NA
- **Tags**: Trajectory Prediction, Generative Model, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对随机物理系统轨迹生成中有限样本导致模型坍缩到均值预测，提出了随机提升（Stochastic Lifting）方法，为每个状态转移附加独立随机标签，使模型能够表示多种可能的下一状态。推理时，每步采样新标签并自动回归滚动，仅用单次网络评估即可生成多样轨迹。

<h3 id="203">[203] Relevance as a Vulnerability: How Web Retrieval Degrades Safety Alignment in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29224](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29224) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Nawal, Manit Baser, Mohan Gurusamy
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, RAG, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 该论文针对网络检索增强的AI智能体削弱了安全对齐，导致对有害请求的遵从性增加，提出了AgentREVEAL诊断框架。研究发现安全来源（如警告页面）反而平均增加25%的有害遵从，并提出相关性是两种脆弱性的共同激活条件。同时发布了包含1405个URL和320个有害行为的HarmURLBench基准。

<h3 id="204">[204] Toward Ethical Facial Age Estimation: A Generalized Zero-Shot Benchmark Without Training on Children's Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29230](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29230) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Caio Petrucci, Leo Sampaio Ferraz Ribeiro, Sandra Avila
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Face Recognition, Zero-Shot Learning, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 该论文针对面部年龄估计训练数据包含未成年人图像引发的伦理问题，提出了一个广义零样本基准，训练时排除18岁以下数据，在零样本下评估儿童表现。评估9种方法发现，所有方法在未见年龄组上性能平均下降46.4%，最高达52.8%，且存在明显的所见类偏差。

<h3 id="205">[205] BlockBatch: Multi-Scale Consensus Decoding for Efficient Diffusion Language Model Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29233](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29233) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaoyou Wu, Cheng-Jhih Shih, Binfei Ji, Yong Liu, Yingyan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, LLM Inference, Parallel Decoding
- **Summary**: 该论文针对扩散语言模型推理中块大小选择的粒度权衡，提出了BlockBatch，一种无需训练的在线推理框架，在批处理前向传递中同时执行多个块大小分支。通过置信度门控的标记合并、领导者同步和周期性刷新，BlockBatch在3个模型和4个数据集上平均减少26.6%的去噪NFE，实现1.33倍端到端加速。

<h3 id="206">[206] Wait! There's a Way Out: A Decision Mechanism for Forecasting Conversational Derailment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29243](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29243) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Laerdon Kim, Vivian Nguyen, Cristian Danescu-Niculescu-Mizil
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Decision Making, Online Learning
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对对话脱轨预测中单纯基于脱轨概率触发警报导致高误报率，提出了结合前向模拟来评估恢复可能性的延迟机制。该机制在保持预测准确性的同时大幅降低了假阳性率，并强调了决策在预测系统中的重要性。

<h3 id="207">[207] OmniRetrieval: Unified Retrieval across Heterogeneous Knowledge Sources</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29250](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29250) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinheon Baek, Soyeong Jeong, Sangwoo Park, Woongyeong Yeo, Minki Kang, Patara Trirat, Heejun Lee, Sung Ju Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Knowledge Graph, RAG
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有检索器在单一数据源上操作，接口碎片化，提出了OmniRetrieval框架，它接受自然语言查询，识别合适的知识源，并分发原生查询到对应的执行引擎。在13个数据集和309个知识库上，OmniRetrieval超越了单一来源基线，展示了作为异构源通用接口的能力。

<h3 id="208">[208] Extreme dynamic symmetry enables omnidirectional and multifunctional robots</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29254](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29254) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaxun Liu, Boxi Xia, Boyuan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Locomotion Control, Control Theory
- **Venue**: Science Robotics (2026)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对机器人设计中对称性主要局限于几何形态，提出了动态对称性（动态各向同性）概念，并开发了Argus系列球形机器人。仿真和实验表明，高动态对称性一致改善了轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性和能效；实际20腿Argus机器人实现了近极限动态各向同性，展示了方向无关的移动、敏捷地形通过、快速自稳定和部分执行器失效下的韧性。

<h3 id="209">[209] DynSess: Dynamic Session-Level Evaluation and Optimization Framework for Role-Playing Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29256](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29256) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rongsheng Zhang, Jiji Tang, Junnan Ren, Zuyi Bao, Weijie Chen, Ruofan Hu, Zhou Zhao, Tangjie Lv, Yan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation, Role-Playing Agent
- **Summary**: 现有角色扮演代理的评估和优化方法仅针对单轮，无法捕捉长对话质量。提出统一的会话级框架DynSess，包括会话级评估DynSess-Eval和两种优化变体（DSPO、GSRPO）。实验表明DynSess-Eval与人类判断更一致，DynSess-Character用更少参数匹配最强模型。

<h3 id="210">[210] KLAS: Using Similarity to Stitch Neural Networks for Improved Accuracy-Efficiency Tradeoffs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29259](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29259) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Debopam Sanyal, Anantharaman Iyer, Alind Khare, Trisha Jain, Akshay Jajoo, Myungjin Lee, Clayton Kerce, Alexey Tumanov
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Transfer Learning, Model Stitching
- **Summary**: 现有模型拼接方法依赖启发式，次优且泛化差。提出KLAS框架，利用KL散度自动选择最佳拼接配置。在ImageNet-1K上，KLAS在相同计算成本下提高准确率1.21%，或保持准确率同时减少1.33倍FLOPs。

<h3 id="211">[211] Compute Allocation in Evolutionary Search: From Depth-Breadth to Multi-Armed Bandits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29268](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29268) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sixue Xing, Haoyu He, Kerui Wu, Zhuo Yang, Haozheng Luo, Tianfan Fu, Aarthy Nagarajan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Evolutionary Algorithm, Multi-Armed Bandit, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: LLM引导的进化搜索运行间分布未知，固定预算分配不明确。提出BaSE，使用多臂赌博机分配LLM调用到并行轨迹。在不改变模型、提示或评估器下，BaSE将平均适应度提高12.3%。

<h3 id="212">[212] Causal Label Recovery in Payment Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29272](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29272) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gaurav Dhama
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Fraud Detection, Missing Data
- **Summary**: 支付网络欺诈检测标签存在授权、报告、延迟和损坏四重偏差。形式化为序列缺失数据问题，构建序列三重稳健（STR）估计器。STR实现半参数效率界，优于朴素标签训练，允许使用更近期的数据。

<h3 id="213">[213] Code-QA-Bench: Separating Code Reasoning from Documentation Memorization in Repository-Level QA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29277](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29277) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jun Zhang, JianYing Qu, Hanwen Du, Zhongkai Sun, Yehua Yang, Qiao Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Repo-Level Code Generation, Question Answering, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 现有仓库级代码QA基准无法区分代码理解与文档记忆。提出自动化框架Code-QA-Bench，包含答案优先生成和三条件实验设计。实验表明代码访问是主要因素（+0.23），文档提供少量增益（+0.071）。

<h3 id="214">[214] LoopFM: Learning frOm HistOrical RePresentations of Foundation Model for Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29280](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29280) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shali Jiang, Hua Zheng, Boyang Liu, Laming Chen, Kenny Lov, Chuanqi Xu, Lisang Ding, Qinghai Zhou, Can Cui, Xiaolong Liu, Xiaoyi Liu, Yasmine Badr, Xin Xu, Jiyan Yang, Ellie Dingqiao Wen, Gerard Jonathan Mugisha Akkerhuis, Chenxiao Guan, Rong Jin, Ruichao Qiu, Xian Chen, Shifu Xu, Zhehui Zhou, Ping Chen, Rui Yang, Haicheng Chen, Xiangge Meng, Song Zhou, Dharak Kharod, Shuyu Xu, Qiang Jin, Qiao Yang, Wankun Zhu, Qin Huang, Yuzhen Huang, Darren Liu, Parish Aggarwal, Hui Zhou, Erzhuo Wang, Shuo Chang, Xiaorui Gan, Wenlin Chen, Santanu Kolay, Huayu Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Recommender System, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 知识蒸馏仅传输标量预测，传输比递减。提出LoopFM，将基础模型中间嵌入作为下游特征，无需实时推理。在淘宝广告上AUC提升6%+，工业部署中转换率提升0.5-1.22%。

<h3 id="215">[215] Do Physics Foundation Models Learn Generalizable Physics? A Bias-Aware Benchmark Across Physical Regimes and Distribution Shifts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29283](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29283) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mengdi Chu, Yang Liu, Ayan Biswas, Han-Wei Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Foundation Model, Benchmark, Distribution Shift
- **Summary**: 物理基础模型评估坍缩为单一平均分数，无法判断泛化性。构建包含8种物理动力学、25个测试体系的基准。结果表明当前模型是条件性泛化者，改进数据分布仅部分缓解。

<h3 id="216">[216] Pocket-Dentist: On-Device Dental Image Understanding via Efficient Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29299](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29299) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kai Bian, Xucheng Guo, Bin Chen, Lingyan Ruan, Yiran Shen, Ting Dang, Hong Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Edge Computing, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 牙科视觉语言模型评估碎片化且忽视计算成本。提出效率感知基准Pocket-Dentist，发现紧凑VLM优于大型VLM。微调后的2B模型在iPhone上每样本4.31秒，延迟降低4.9倍，内存减少2.3倍。

<h3 id="217">[217] MusTBENCH: Benchmarking and Advancing Temporal Grounding in Music LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29300](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29300) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daeyong Kwon, Qiyu Wu, Shinobu Kuriya, Junghyun Koo, Shuyang Cui, Zhi Zhong, Wei-Hsiang Liao, Hiromi Wakaki, Yuki Mitsufuji
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Music Understanding, Audio-Language Model, Temporal Grounding, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 音频大语言模型在音乐理解中缺乏时间定位能力。提出MusTBENCH基准和MusT四阶段优化方案。现有LALM时间定位困难，MusT带来显著改进。

<h3 id="218">[218] GrepSeek: Training Search Agents for Direct Corpus Interaction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29307](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29307) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alireza Salemi, Chang Zeng, Atharva Nijasure, Jui-Hui Chung, Razieh Rahimi, Fernando Diaz, Hamed Zamani
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Information Retrieval, Question Answering, Search Agent
- **Summary**: 现有搜索代理依赖检索器，缺乏直接语料交互。提出GrepSeek，训练紧凑搜索代理通过shell命令搜索，结合两阶段训练和分片并行引擎。在7个开放域QA基准上取得最强F1和EM，加速7.6倍。

<h3 id="219">[219] Rethinking FID Through the Geometry of the Reference Dataset</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29335](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29335) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yunghee Lee, Byeonghyun Pak
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Generation, Image Quality Assessment, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop: Combining Theory and Benchmarks
- **Summary**: FID低不总是对应好质量，取决于参考数据集几何。在6个数据集上研究分布密度和有效秩的影响。集中数据集产生更有利FID趋势，分散数据集可能使FID变差。

<h3 id="220">[220] Does Distributed Training Undermine Compute Governance?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29359](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29359) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Robi Rahman
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Distributed Computing, AI Safety, AI Governance, Distributed Training
- **Summary**: 分布式训练可能规避计算治理监管。评估规避可行性并提出反制措施，包括举报、芯片追踪、法务审计等。

<h3 id="221">[221] SURGENT: A Surgical Multi-Agent Assistance System Across the Perioperative Workflow</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29368](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29368) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongsheng Shi, Yue Li, Xin Yi, Yongyi Cui, Huawei Feng, Linlin Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multi-Agent System, RAG, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 现有LLM不适用于外科，输入长度限制、记忆有限。提出SURGENT，包含树状思考规划器、多部门协作代理和检索增强推理。在五个围手术期任务上优于基线，可用DeepSeek本地部署保护隐私。

<h3 id="222">[222] TRACER: Persistent Regularization for Robust Multimodal Finetuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29380](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29380) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hesam Asadollahzadeh, Feng Liu, Christopher Leckie, Sarah M. Erfani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Contrastive Learning, Robustness, Fine-Tuning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HesamAsad/TRACER)
- **Summary**: 微调多模态模型会降低分布外鲁棒性。理论分析后提出TRACER，结合对比学习和加权移动平均教师蒸馏。在CLIP微调上持续改进OOD准确率和校准。

<h3 id="223">[223] Latent Terms: Dense Retrievers Contain Trivially Extractable BM25-ready Zipfian Vocabularies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29384](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29384) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Benjamin Clavié, Sean Lee, Aamir Shakir, Makoto P. Kato
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Sparse Retrieval, Sparse Autoencoder
- **Summary**: 稠密检索器内部结构未被充分利用。使用稀疏自动编码器提取潜在词汇，直接用于BM25。匹配或超越单向量评分方法，在LIMIT任务上显著优于基础模型。

<h3 id="224">[224] On the Optimizer Dependence of Neural Scaling Laws</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29387](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29387) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vansh Ramani, Shourya Vir Jain
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Optimization, Scaling Laws, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 神经缩放定律的指数α通常视为常数，但可能依赖于优化器。在随机特征回归实验中测量α。预条件优化器产生更陡峭缩放，自然梯度α是梯度下降的2.6倍。

<h3 id="225">[225] GDSD: Reinforcement Learning as Guided Denoiser Self-Distillation for Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29398](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29398) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaohang Tang, Keyue Jiang, Che Liu, Qifang Zhao, Xiaoxiao Xu, Sangwoong Yoon, Ilija Bogunovic
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Knowledge Distillation, Diffusion Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/GaryBall/GDSD)
- **Summary**: 强化学习用于扩散语言模型时，ELBO近似存在训练-推理偏差问题。本文提出引导去噪自蒸馏（GDSD）方法，通过优势引导的自教师直接蒸馏去噪器，无需ELBO。在规划、数学和编程任务上，GDSD相比现有ELBO方法准确率提升高达19.6%。

<h3 id="226">[226] Semantic and Visual Evidence for Efficient Long-Video Reasoning: A Solution for the HD-EPIC VQA Challenge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29402](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29402) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yinsong Xu, Wei Jing, Liuxin Zhang, Wanjun Lv, Hui Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Vision-Language Model, Long Context
- **Summary**: 长视频理解面临上下文长度和细粒度视觉细节不足的挑战。本文提出将长视频推理解耦为语义证据和视觉证据，并通过查询条件检索动态整合。在HD-EPIC VQA挑战中取得竞争性结果。

<h3 id="227">[227] The Good, the Bad, and the Ugly of Markov Boundary for Tabular Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29411](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29411) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shu Wan, Abhinav Gorantla, Huan Liu, K. Selçuk Candan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ME; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Tabular Learning
- **Summary**: 马尔可夫边界理论上提供最优特征子集，但实际预测中从因果发现恢复的边界效果不佳。本文在合成基准上分析发现，发现算法优化目标与预测不匹配，且错误代价不对称。结论是对齐预测的特征选择方法更有效。

<h3 id="228">[228] Beyond Bilingual Transfer: Multilingual Code-Switching in Instruction Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29414](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29414) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shunta Asano, Jeonghun Baek, Toshihiko Yamasaki
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multilingual Learning, Instruction Tuning, Code-Switching
- **Summary**: 现有代码混合研究仅限于双语，多语言场景未探索。本文在四种语言上进行多语言代码混合指令微调。实验表明简单的句子级多语言代码混合一致提升所有语言的性能。

<h3 id="229">[229] DELOS: Detecting Shallow Transits in Kepler Photometry Using a Contrastive-Learning Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29428](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29428) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qingtian Liu, Jian Ge, XingChen Yan, Kevin Willis, Xinyu Yao, QuanQuan Hu, Jiapeng Zhu
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.EP; astro-ph.IM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Classification, Contrastive Learning, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 开普勒光度数据中浅层凌星信号检测困难。本文提出基于对比学习的DELOS框架，结合GPU加速折叠和1D卷积编码器。在低信噪比区域，相比BLS和TLS，精确率-召回率提升15.5%和11.25%，速度提升3-80倍。

<h3 id="230">[230] AliMark: Enhancing Robustness of Sentence-Level Watermarking Against Text Paraphrasing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29434](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29434) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuexin Li, Wenjie Qu, Linyu Wu, Yulin Chen, Yufei He, Tri Cao, Bryan Hooi, Jiaheng Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Text Watermarking, Adversarial Robustness
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 现有句子级水印方法对句子拆分合并等结构扰动鲁棒性不足。本文提出AliMark，将水印重构为比特序列对齐问题，采用多候选检测策略。在多种改写攻击下显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="231">[231] SkillBrew: Multi-Objective Curation of Skill Banks for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29440](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29440) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wentao Hu, Zhendong Chu, Yiming Zhang, Junda Wu, Ming Jin, Xiangyu Zhao, Yilei Shao, Yanfeng Wang, Qingsong Wen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Objective Optimization, Skill Learning
- **Summary**: 检索增强的LLM智能体依赖技能库，但现有方法不断添加技能导致冗余。本文提出SkillBrew，将技能库整理形式化为多目标Pareto优化问题，通过双层提出-验证循环求解。在两个基准上验证有效性。

<h3 id="232">[232] How Coding Agents Fail Their Users: A Large-Scale Analysis of Developer-Agent Misalignment in 20,574 Real-World Sessions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29442](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29442) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ningzhi Tang, Chaoran Chen, Gelei Xu, Yiyu Shi, Yu Huang, Collin McMillan, Tao Dong, Toby Jia-Jun Li
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Agent, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Summary**: 编码智能体与开发者之间的失调未得到充分分析。本文对20,574个真实会话进行观察研究，识别出七种失调形式。90.50%的失调造成努力和信任成本，91.49%需要用户显式纠正。

<h3 id="233">[233] How Much Is a Dataset Worth? Scaling Laws, the Vendi Score, and Matrix Spectral Functions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29448](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29448) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jeff A. Bilmes, Gantavya Bhatt, Arnav M. Das
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Data Valuation, Data Selection, Scaling Laws
- **Summary**: 数据价值评估需超越数据集规模。本文证明Vendi Score和神经缩放律均具有次模性，引入矩阵谱函数族，并提出高效优化方法。实验发现在多个数据集中，设施定位函数预测性能最佳。

<h3 id="234">[234] Forget Less, Generalize More: Unifying Temporal and Structural Adaptation for Dynamic Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29453](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29453) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qian Chang, Ciprian Doru Giurcaneanu, Runsong Jia, Xia Li, Guoping Hu, Xiufeng Cheng, Jinqing Yang, Mengjia Wu, Yi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dynamic Graph, Temporal Learning, Graph Neural Network
- **Summary**: 动态图表示学习现有方法采用固定时间衰减，泛化性差。本文提出双尺度保持动力学（DSRD），包含双尺度自适应保持状态和自适应衰减核。在14个基准上达到链接预测和节点分类的最优性能。

<h3 id="235">[235] Adaptive Interviewing for Persona Simulation in LLMs: Evidence-Grounded Reasoning Improves Decision Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29458](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29458) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruoxi Su, Yuhan Liu, Jingyu Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Persona, Dialogue System, Social Simulation
- **Summary**: 静态人物描述不足以准确模拟个体决策。本文提出自适应访谈框架，通过结构化三阶段对话收集人物相关信息。实验发现只有当模型基于用户特定证据进行推理时，决策对齐才提升。

<h3 id="236">[236] Benchmarking Large Vision-Language Models on CFMME: A Comprehensive Chinese Financial Multimodal Evaluation Dataset</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29462](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29462) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qian Chen, Xianyin Zhang, Yanzhi Liu, Lifan Guo, Feng Chen, Chi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Financial AI, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 中文金融领域多模态评估缺乏。本文构建CFMME基准，包含6,052个实例覆盖8种图像模态和4类任务。最佳模型在问答任务上准确率仅66.11%，表明巨大改进空间。

<h3 id="237">[237] Honest Lying: Understanding Memory Confabulation in Reflexive Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29463](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29463) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Prakhar Dixit, Sadia Kamal, Tim Oates
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Self-Correction
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop "Failure Modes in Agentic AI"
- **Summary**: Reflexion类智能体依赖自我反思作为记忆，但可能存储错误解释。本文定义记忆虚构现象，提出反思重复率（RRR）进行检测。通过程序化提取失败信号，正确目标提及率从0%提升至86%。

<h3 id="238">[238] Composing Non-Conjugate Factor Graphs with Closed-Form Variational Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29467](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29467) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mykola Lukashchuk, Kyrylo Yemets, Wouter M. Kouw, Dmitry Bagaev, İsmail Şenöz, Jeff Beck, Bert de Vries
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Variational Inference, Time Series Forecasting, Bayesian Inference
- **Summary**: 堆叠概率构建模块通常破坏闭式推理。本文识别五种因子图原语，证明其保持闭式变分消息传递。通过堆叠路由层编码决策树，实现通用函数逼近，在时间序列集成预测中提供校准的不确定性。

<h3 id="239">[239] SciIntBench: Measuring LLM Compliance with Research Integrity Norms Under Adversarial Framing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29468](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29468) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Almene De Meran Meguimtsop, Maria Leonor Pacheco, Daniel E. Acuna
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Red Teaming, AI Safety, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型在科研诚信中的合规性未充分评估。本文构建SciIntBench对抗基准，含810个提示覆盖10类科研诚信范畴。实验发现模型对显式不当行为拒绝可靠，但对隐蔽违规拒绝较弱。

<h3 id="240">[240] Inform, Coach, Relate, Listen: Auditing LLM Caregiving Support Roles</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29473](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29473) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Drishti Goel, Agam Goyal, Veda Duddu, Olivia Pal, Jeongah Lee, Qiuyue Joy Zhong, Violeta J. Rodriguez, Daniel S. Brown, Dong Whi Yoo, Ravi Karkar, Koustuv Saha
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.SI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, AI Safety, Dialogue System
- **Summary**: 语言模型在照护对话中的安全性因支持角色而异。本文基于社会支持理论定义四种角色，并在5,000个真实查询上评估。发现信息导向角色虽被认为更可靠，但展现出更高的交互风险。

<h3 id="241">[241] MOOSE-Copilot: A Web-Based Interactive Assistant for Unified Exploratory and Fine-Grained Scientific Hypothesis Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29475) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongran An, Zonglin Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Scientific Reasoning, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 (System Demonstrations)
- **Summary**: 现有的LLM假设发现方法将探索性构思和精细优化视为孤立任务，且缺乏人类指导。本文提出MOOSE-Copilot，首个统一框架，通过形式化人机交互协议让科学家通过蓝图、路由和反馈引导生成过程。结果表明注入结构化专家信号显著优于纯自主基线，降低了复杂命令行的学习门槛。

<h3 id="242">[242] Evolutionary Rule Extraction from Corporate Default Prediction Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29478](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29478) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Desirè Fabbretti, Matteo Pasquino, Elia Pacioni, Caterina Lucarelli, Davide Calvaresi
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Financial AI, Evolutionary Algorithm, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 机器学习模型提高了企业违约预测性能，但缺乏可解释性。本文提出DEXiRE-EVO，结合多目标优化和CIU可解释性方法的进化规则抽取框架。实验表明该框架优于逻辑回归，抽取的规则揭示了与财务困境相关的经济意义模式。

<h3 id="243">[243] PhoneWorld: Scaling Phone-Use Agent Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29486](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29486) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhengyang Tang, Yuxuan Liu, Xin Lai, Junyi Li, Pengyuan Lyu, Jason, Yiduo Guo, Zhengyao Fang, Yang Ding, Yi Zhang, Weinong Wang, Huawen Shen, Xingran Zhou, Liang Wu, Fei Tang, Sunqi Fan, Shangpin Peng, Zheng Ruan, Anran Zhang, Benyou Wang, Rui Yan, Ji-Rong Wen, Chengquan Zhang, Han Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: GUI Automation, Mobile AI, Environment Generation
- **Summary**: 构建可控、可复现的手机使用环境难以规模化。本文提出PhoneWorld流水线，将真实GUI轨迹转化为可控环境、任务、验证器和训练数据。在固定训练预算下，PhoneWorld监督同时提升了四个评测基准，证明了其扩展性。

<h3 id="244">[244] AnyMo: Scaling Any-Modality Conditional Motion Generation with Masked Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29488](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29488) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 条件人体运动生成受限于固定模态配置和稀缺的多模态数据。本文构建OmniHuMo大规模数据集并提出AnyMo框架，采用Residual FSQ运动分词器和可扩展的掩码建模Transformer，支持任意模态组合的高质量运动合成。

<h3 id="245">[245] The New Pro Se: Generative AI and the Surge in Federal Civil Self-Representation</h3>

- **Authors**: Or Cohen-Sasson
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Legal AI, AI-Generated Content, Social Science
- **Summary**: 自生成式AI普及后，联邦民事诉讼中自诉原告比例显著上升。本文分析约28万份诉状，提出AI一致性起草测量方法。结果发现AI标记诉状引用更密集，多为首次起诉者，但更易被驳回，区分了法律形式与实效。

<h3 id="246">[246] Quotient DAGs for Off-Policy Evaluation:Forward-Flow Importance Sampling and Exact Slate Propensities</h3>

- **Summary**: 基于自回归列表推荐器的离线策略评估存在方差和计算效率问题。本文提出商DAG视角和Forward-DP算法，精确计算无序列表倾向性，避免阶乘枚举，实现了实用的倾向性评估和模型选择。

<h3 id="247">[247] Source-Grounded Semantic Reinforcement Learning for Low-Resource Target-Language Generation</h3>

<h3 id="248">[248] Network Optimization Aspects of Autonomous Vehicles: Challenges and Future Directions</h3>

<h3 id="249">[249] KBF: Knowledge Boundary as Fingerprint for Language Model and Black-Box API Auditing</h3>

<h3 id="250">[250] Temporal Motif-aware Graph Test-time Adaptation for OOD Blockchain Anomaly Detection</h3>

<h3 id="251">[251] GUITestScape: Towards Open-set Evaluation on Exploratory GUI Testing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29532](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29532) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="252">[252] GiPL: Generative augmented iterative Pseudo-Labeling for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Object Detection</h3>

<h3 id="253">[253] SCOPE: A Lightweight-training LLM Framework for Air Traffic Control Readback Monitoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29543](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29543) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="254">[254] Singularity-aware Optimization via Randomized Geometric Probing: Towards Stable Non-smooth Optimization</h3>

<h3 id="255">[255] VLA-Pro: Cross-Task Procedural Memory Transfer for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>

<h3 id="256">[256] Brain-IT-VQA: From Brain Signals to Answers</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; q-bio.NC

<h3 id="257">[257] Training Deliberative Monitors for Black-Box Scheming Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29601](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29601) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Sinha, Akshat Naik, Victor Gillioz, Simon Storf, Kilian Merkelbach, Rich Barton-Cooper, Axel Højmark, Marius Hobbhahn
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, LLM Evaluation, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 现有监控器依赖链式思维或内部激活，成本高或不可用。本文提出训练小型开放权重模型，通过规划规范蒸馏前沿模型推理，结合监督微调和强化学习，在无推理访问条件下检测代理轨迹中的欺骗行为。实验表明，Qwen3.5-27B模型性能优于低成本前沿模型，且推理成本远低于高性能前沿模型，处于成本和性能的帕累托前沿。

<h3 id="258">[258] Learning Context-Conditioned Predicate Semantics via Prototype Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29610](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29610) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 场景图生成中谓词语义随上下文变化。本文提出AlignG，通过原型反馈学习上下文条件化的谓词语义，利用关系候选推断语义并反馈校准表示，同时锚定全局语义中心防止漂移。在VG-150和GQA-200数据集上，SGDet任务的F@100分别提升1.4和2.7。

<h3 id="259">[259] DLM-SWAI: Steering Diffusion Language Models Before They Unmask</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29626](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29626) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyeseon An, Yo-Sub Han
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Language Model, Text Generation
- **Summary**: 扩散语言模型生成可控性缺乏无需重训练的方法。本文提出DLM-SWAI，一种无训练方法，在去噪步骤中利用预计算的词级风格分数偏置令牌分布。实验表明该方法有效控制风格和安全属性，同时保持生成质量，并揭示强度与流畅性的可调权衡。

<h3 id="260">[260] COMET: Concept Space Dissection of the Modality Gap in Audio-Text Multimodal Contrastive Embeddings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29628](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29628) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yonggang Zhu, Liting Gao, Aidong Men, Wenwu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Audio-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Contrastive Learning
- **Summary**: 音频-文本对比嵌入中的模态间隙问题，现有解释不充分。本文提出COMET框架，通过PLS-SVD分解发现仅有少量可解释轴贡献于相似度计算，并提出频谱截断方法。该方法在零样本音频字幕中接近全监督性能，且无需额外存储或计算。

<h3 id="261">[261] Entity-Collision: A Stratified Protocol for Attributing Retrieval Lift in Agent Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29630](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29630) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 端到端代理记忆基准混淆了词汇泄漏和标签混合。本文提出实体碰撞协议，通过控制实体重叠和分层查询，隔离嵌入器贡献。在多个标签和嵌入器上揭示两轴模式，并发现编码器容量并非主要限制因素。

<h3 id="262">[262] Predicting Causal Effects from Natural Language Queries using Structured Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29631](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29631) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Giuliano Martinelli, Piriyakorn Piriyatamwong, Abelardo Carlos Martinez Lorenzo, Jasmin Baier, Riccardo Orlando, Satvik Garg, Sharif Kazemi, Linxi Wang, Arianna Legovini, Samuel Fraiberger
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Benchmark, Natural Language Understanding
- **Summary**: 从现有实验证据预测因果效应大小，现有LLM方法效果有限。本文引入Query2Effect基准（72k问题），并提出两步框架：先生成结构化表示，再用编码器预测效应大小。微调后绝对误差降低27%-71%，两步框架有助于域外泛化。

<h3 id="263">[263] The Sample Complexity of Multiclass and Sparse Contextual Bandits</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29645](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29645) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Contextual Bandits, Sample Complexity
- **Summary**: 稀疏奖励的上下文多臂赌博机样本复杂度问题，现有工作上界有Θ(\|A\|^9)额外依赖。本文提出基于决策估计系数和低方差探索的算法，达到最优样本复杂度O((s/ε^2 + \|A\|/ε)log\|Π\|)，并给出匹配下界。

<h3 id="264">[264] OccamToken: Efficient VLM Inference with Training-Free and Budget-Adaptive Token Pruning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29657](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29657) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Token Pruning, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 视觉语言模型预填充阶段因长视觉令牌序列而昂贵，现有绝对排名剪枝易受注意力沉点影响。本文提出OccamToken，一种无训练框架，通过寄存器锚定相对证据测试进行令牌剪枝。在LLaVA-NeXT上将2880个令牌压缩至约40个，保留93%以上准确率。

<h3 id="265">[265] Opir: Efficient Multi-Task Safety Classification for Toxicity, Jailbreaks, Hate Speech, and Harmful Content</h3>

<h3 id="266">[266] EviLink: Multi-Path Schema Linking with Uncertainty-Guided Evidence Acquisition for Large-Scale Text-to-SQL</h3>

- **Summary**: 大规模文本到SQL中模式链接通常视为单路径确定性选择，忽视多路径不确定性。本文提出EviLink，结合多假设模式基础和不确定性引导的证据获取。在Spider2-Snow上达到90.15%字段级严格召回率，平均令牌数123.30K，提升下游SQL生成。

<h3 id="267">[267] From Prompts to Context: An Ontology-Driven Framework for Human-Generative AI Collaboration</h3>

<h3 id="268">[268] Personalized Turn-Level User Conversation Satisfaction Benchmark</h3>

<h3 id="269">[269] Teaching Language Models to Check Grounded Claim Factuality with Human Test-Taking Strategies</h3>

<h3 id="270">[270] The Little Book of Generative AI Foundations: An Intuitive Mathematical Primer</h3>

<h3 id="271">[271] Multi-Legal-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Legal Reasoning Across Jurisdictions, Languages, and Legal Traditions</h3>

<h3 id="273">[273] Energy-Aware NECO for Single-Pass Pixel-wise Out-of-Distribution Detection in Semantic Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29773](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29773) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Boyuan Zhang, Huanshan Huang, Yifei Cao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Out-of-Distribution Detection, Semantic Segmentation
- **Venue**: ICRA 2026 Workshop on Long-term Deployments in the Wild (LoWi 2026)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/boyuan-zhangx/Energy-Aware_NECO)
- **Summary**: 针对语义分割中分布外检测需要多次随机前向传播难以在边缘平台部署的问题，提出Energy-Aware NECO，一种单次前向的像素级OOD检测器，结合中心化NECO几何比率和logit能量分数，通过凸融合得到混合分数。在miniMUAD数据集上AUROC达到0.8539，优于单独使用NECO或能量分数以及集成预测熵基线，同时保持单次前向的高效性。

<h3 id="274">[274] Hista and Numca: Estimate State Value Effectively for LLM Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29782](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29782) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zizhe Chen, Jiqian Dong, Yizhou Tian, Garry Yang, Yongqiang Chen, Zhitang Chen, James Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Value Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM强化学习中状态值估计不准确导致训练不稳定的问题，提出Numca（利用数值跨度作为梯度里程碑）和Hista（利用隐藏状态加权平均不连续轨迹的回报）两种技术。在SVEB基准上，两种方法均能提高状态值估计精度，并提升不同RL算法和模型大小的训练效果，且计算开销小。

<h3 id="275">[275] Evolve as a Team: Collaborative Self-Evolution for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29790](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29790) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhezheng Hao, Tianfu Wang, Huanshuo Dong, Ziyan Liu, Hong Wang, Xiankun Lin, Qiang Lin, Can Wang, Hande Dong, Jiawei Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对LLM多智能体系统执行失败难以消除且经验驱动进化困难的问题，提出Meta-Team框架，基于协作自我进化，通过保存执行上下文和协调后任务通信，实现多尺度自我进化（行为、协调、组织）。在六个长周期任务基准上一致优于单智能体、手工MAS和先前MAS进化方法。

<h3 id="276">[276] Data filtering methods for training language models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29807](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29807) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Egor Shevchenko, Elena Bruches
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Data Quality, Label Noise, Text Classification
- **Summary**: 针对训练数据中标签错误降低模型泛化能力的问题，比较了Confident Learning和Dataset Cartography两种自动标签错误检测方法在三个俄语文本分类语料上的效果。实验表明，方法有效性依赖数据集特征：大语料低噪声时过滤无改善，小语料高噪声时Confident Learning显著提升F1宏观分数，且两种方法的有目标移除均优于随机移除。

<h3 id="277">[277] Inferring Code Correctness from Specification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29822](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29822) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tambon Florian, Papadakis Mike
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Program Verification
- **Summary**: 针对LLM生成代码的正确性验证依赖动态共识或静态推理且成本高的问题，提出TRAILS方法，基于规格生成多样测试输入，执行后让LLM评估输入-输出对是否符合规格而不看代码。在LiveCodeBench和CoCoClaNeL上，TRAILS相比零样本思维链和HoarePrompt，马修斯相关系数提升最多39%，且稳定性更好。

<h3 id="278">[278] Towards Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing for Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29826](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29826) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Leijiang Gu, Zhen Zeng, Feng Li, Xinjian Gao, Zenglin Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Editing, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对多模态知识编辑中因果错位和特征纠缠导致编辑无法泛化到逻辑相关查询并破坏无关信息的问题，提出LDKE框架，通过定位事实特定模型层和分离目标相关与无关输入实现精确泛化编辑。在多种MLLM和基准上，LDKE在传播编辑到相关上下文同时保持高局部性方面表现优异。

<h3 id="279">[279] CB-SLICE: Concept-Based Interpretable Error Slice Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29836](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29836) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yael Konforti, Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga, Elaf Almahmoud, Mateja Jamnik
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Concept Bottleneck Model
- **Venue**: Proceedings of the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
- **Summary**: 针对现有错误片段发现方法提供解释与模型推理过程脱节的问题，提出CB-SLICE，基于概念瓶颈模型，利用概念预测失败分组样本并识别关键概念。在多个基准上优于现有方法，提供更丰富且忠实的模型错误解释。

<h3 id="280">[280] HARP: Hadamard-Preconditioned Adaptive Rotation Processor for Extreme LLM Quantization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29843](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29843) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Artur Zagitov, Gleb Molodtsov, Aleksandr Beznosikov
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantization, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 针对极端低比特量化对激活异常值和权重曲率敏感的问题，提出HARP，一种可学习的结构化双面正交处理器，替代固定哈达玛变换，通过蝶形块正交阶段表示旋转，支持非2幂维度。在1B-70B模型上2-4比特设置下，HARP提升困惑度和零样本准确率，且推理速度达128 tok/s vs FP16的61 tok/s。

<h3 id="281">[281] ESPO: Early-Stopping Proximal Policy Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29860](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29860) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zihang Li, Rui Zhou, Yingcheng Shi, Wenhan Yu, Zhewen Tan, Zixiang Liu, Zeming Li, Binhua Li, Yongbin Li, Tong Yang, Jieping Ye
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对LLM强化学习中早期错误步骤浪费计算资源并污染优势估计的问题，提出ESPO，在线检测轨迹失败并提前终止，将截断轨迹视为吸收失败状态。在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B数学推理训练中，ESPO在AIME 2024、AMC 2023和MATH-500上超过PPO，同时节省20%以上生成token。

<h3 id="282">[282] Towards Verifiable Multimodal Deep Research: A Multi-Agent Harness for Interleaved Report Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29861) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chenghao Zhang, Guanting Dong, Yufan Liu, Tong Zhao, Zhicheng Dou
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multimodal Learning, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 针对可验证多模态深度研究中开放式合成缺乏确定性真值且需要交错文本与视觉证据的问题，提出Ptah多智能体框架，通过规划、研究和写作阶段，专用智能体构建视觉感知计划、收集基于声明的证据、维护源对齐图像，并通过验证器确保事实和引用。在深度研究基准上，Ptah生成更可靠、视觉信息丰富且可用的人类面向多模态报告。

<h3 id="283">[283] Mitigating Stethoscope-Induced Shortcuts in Respiratory Sound Classification under Federated Domain Generalization with Causality-Inspired Interventions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29862](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29862) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Heejoon Koo, Yoon Tae Kim, Miika Toikkanen, June-Woo Kim
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Domain Generalization, Audio Processing, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 针对听诊器差异导致多站点呼吸音分类性能下降的问题，提出因果启发的多模态联邦域泛化框架，包含设备风格干预网络、反事实文本增强和梯度对齐。在ICBHI和SPRSound数据集上的留一设备验证中，优于传统数据增强和联邦学习基线。

<h3 id="284">[284] Evolutionary Dynamics of Cooperation in Next-Generation LLM Agent Systems: A Cross-Provider Empirical Extension</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29874](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29874) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Francisco León Zúñiga Bolívar
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.GT
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Game Theory
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/arqFranciscoLeon/evollm)
- **Summary**: 针对下一代LLM智能体是否继承前代合作偏差的问题，扩展基准到四个前沿模型，采用相同协议比较三种提示风格和四种群体组成。发现合作偏差普遍存在（9/12模型-提示组合），但跨提供者差异较大（Gemini 2.5 Flash达77%攻击平衡，GPT-5.4 Mini达70%合作均衡）。提供者身份而非模型代际是均衡结果的最强关联因素。

<h3 id="285">[285] Mitigating Hallucination in Vision-Language Models through Barrier-Regulated Adaptive Closed-form Steering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29881](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29881) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Soumyadeep Jana, Pulkit Mittal, Sanasam Ranbir Singh
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型解码过程中视觉接地减弱导致幻觉的问题，提出BRACS训练无关框架，通过监测注意力测量视觉接地，仅在接地恶化时以闭式解修正隐藏状态。在LLaVA-1.5-7B和Qwen-VL-Chat上，CHAIRs降低9.4点，POPE F1提升2.7点，速度达贪婪解码的80%。

<h3 id="286">[286] CRITIC-R1: Learning Structured Critics for Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29886](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29886) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenhan Xiao, Ziwei Zhang, Chuanyue Yu, Xingcheng Fu, Qingyun Sun, Runhua Xu, Jianxin Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对RAG中现有外部批评提供粗粒度反馈且过度干预导致不可靠修正的问题，提出CRITIC-R1结构化批评框架，将RAG批评转化为显式错误诊断，包含判决、错误定位、推理分析和修复生成，使用GRPO和过程级监督训练。在五个QA基准上一致提升答案质量。

<h3 id="287">[287] LaRA: Layer-wise Representation Analysis for Detecting Data Contamination in RL Post-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29888](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29888) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Minju Gwak, Minseo Kwak, Dongseok Lee, Guijin Son, Alan Ritter, Jaehyung Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark Contamination, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 针对RL后训练中数据污染检测方法依赖输出信号（似然/熵）不可靠的问题，提出LaRA层表示分析框架，引入三种度量（扰动敏感性、方向坍塌、局部刚性）。在RL训练推理模型上，基于层偏差的聚合协议优于现有输出级基线。

<h3 id="288">[288] Internal Representation, Not Clinical Knowledge: Where Apparent LLM Triage Failures Originate</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29889](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29889) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David Fraile Navarro, Berardino Como, Jialei Sheng, Soundariya Ananthan, Shlomo Berkovsky
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Medical AI, Sparse Autoencoder
- **Summary**: 针对临床分诊基准中LLM在约束多选输出下报告高漏诊率而自由文本下得分不同的现象，探究输出格式是否改变临床表示。使用稀疏自编码器特征分析发现，相同医疗特征在两种格式下激活，但在多选决策token处沉默。行为上，多选惩罚在结构化和自然语言输入下均反转，且错误多为邻近选择而非知识失败。因此失败源于输出格式而非临床表示。

<h3 id="289">[289] Agora: Toward Autonomous Bug Detection in Production-Level Consensus Protocols with LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29910](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29910) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiang Liu, Sa Song, Zhaowei Zhang, Huiying Lan, Jason Zeng, Ming Wu, Michael Heinrich, Yong Sun, Ceyao Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Software Testing
- **Summary**: 针对共识协议中深层逻辑错误的检测难题，提出领域感知的多智能体框架Agora，通过假设驱动测试和专用智能体协作探索协议状态空间。在四种共识实现中发现15个先前未知的逻辑错误，而现有LLM方法未能检测任何此类错误。

<h3 id="290">[290] Selection Hyper-heuristics Can Automatically Adjust the Learning Period to Optimally Solve Pseudo-Boolean Problems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29916](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29916) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Benjamin Doerr, Pietro S. Oliveto, John Alasdair Warwicker
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.DS; math.OC
- **Tags**: Hyper-Heuristic, Optimization, Evolutionary Algorithm
- **Venue**: Artificial Intelligence
- **Summary**: 针对超启发式算法中学习周期参数难以自动调节的问题，证明了一种选择超启发式算法能够自动设置学习周期。实现了LeadingOnes基准问题的最优时间优化。

<h3 id="291">[291] Does The Way You Plan Matter? An Empirical Study of Planning Representations for LLM Web Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29927](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29927) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alejandra Zambrano, Sara Vera Marjanovic, Imene Kerboua, Xing Han Lù, Leila Kosseim
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Web Agent, Automated Planning
- **Summary**: 针对LLM网页智能体计划表示形式的影响尚未被探索的问题，引入PlanAhead框架评估四种计划表示。结果表明计划制定方式和底层LLM显著影响稳健性和任务成功率。

<h3 id="292">[292] Label Over Logic? How Source Cues Bias Human Fallacy Judgments More Than LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29928](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29928) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mahjabin Nahar, Nafis Irtiza Tripto, Aiping Xiong, Ting-Hao `Kenneth&#39; Huang, Dongwon Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Bias Mitigation, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 研究来源标签对人类和LLM推理判断的影响。实验发现人类对标注为人类或人类辅助AI的谬误更敏感，而LLM的评估相对稳定。

<h3 id="293">[293] CityGen: Structure-Guided City-Style Synthesis for Cross-City Autonomous Driving</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对跨城市自动驾驶中的域偏移问题，提出CityGen扩散生成框架，通过高清地图条件合成实现零标签城市适应。实验证明在感知、分割和规划任务上持续提升跨城市鲁棒性。

<h3 id="294">[294] HoliTok:A Coutinuous Holistic Tokenization with Robust Dual Capabilities of Speech Generation and Understanding</h3>

<h3 id="295">[295] Hijacking Agent Memory: Stealthy Trojan Attacks Through Conversational Interaction</h3>

<h3 id="296">[296] Honeyval: A Comprehensive Evaluation Framework for LLM-powered HTTP Honeypots</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29963](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29963) (cross-listed)

- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Cybersecurity, Intrusion Detection
- **Summary**: 针对LLM驱动蜜罐缺乏统一评估框架的问题，提出Honeyval框架。实验表明LLM蜜罐能显著延长与攻击者的交互时间，且不易被检测。

<h3 id="297">[297] Evaluating Skill and Stability of ArchesWeather and ArchesWeatherGen under Multi-Decadal Climate Simulations</h3>
