<h3 id="300">[300] VisualThink-VLA: Visual Intermediate Reasoning for Effective and Low-Latency Vision-Language-Action Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30011](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30011) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingjian Gao, Wenqiao Zhang, Yuqian Yuan, Yang Dai, Binhe Yu, Zheqi Lv, Haoyu Zheng, Jiaqi Zhu, Zhiqi Ge, Zixuan Wan, Siliang Tang, Yueting Zhuang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Embodied AI, Robotics
- **Summary**: 针对VLA策略中文本思维链延迟高的问题，提出视觉中间推理框架VISUALTHINK-VLA。在多个基准上实现最高成功率，同时将延迟从8.377秒降至0.367秒。

<h3 id="301">[301] Test Time Training for Supervised Causal Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30015](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30015) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zizhen Deng, Jiaru Zhang, Rui Ding, Huang Bojun, Jinzhuo Wang, Qiang Fu, Shi Han, Dongmei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Test-Time Adaptation, Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 针对监督因果学习在分布偏移和组合泛化上的局限性，提出测试时训练框架TTT-SCL。在合成和真实数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="302">[302] Give it Space! Explicit Disentangling of Positional and Semantic Representations in Encoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30022](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30022) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pierre-Antoine Lequeu, Camille Barboule, Benjamin Piwowarski
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Position Encoding, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对Transformer中位置信息处理机制不明确的问题，显式解耦语义、绝对位置和相对位置三个流。解耦方法在语言学探测基准的65个现象中49个有提升。

<h3 id="303">[303] Audio Jailbreaks in Large Audio-Language Models: Taxonomy, Attack-Defense Analysis, and Cost-Aware Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30031](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30031) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bo-Han Feng, Yu-Hsuan Li Liang, Chien-Feng Liu, You-Hsuan Chang, Yun-Nung Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Audio-Language Model, LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack
- **Summary**: 针对大型音频语言模型越狱风险缺乏统一分类和评估的问题，提出统一分类法和受控实验。发现声学最佳-N和叙事框架分别揭示强脆弱性和低延迟威胁。

<h3 id="304">[304] Alignment-Guided Score Matching for Text-to-Image Alignment in Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30038](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30038) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jaa-Yeon Lee, Yeobin Hong, Taesung Kwon, Jong Chul Ye
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Text-to-Image, Contrastive Learning
- **Summary**: 针对扩散模型文本-图像对齐不精确的问题，提出无需奖励的后训练方法，将对比对齐引导集成到分数匹配目标中。在GenEval上计数准确性提升35%以上。

<h3 id="305">[305] Token Inflation: How Dishonest Providers Can Overcharge for Large Language Model Usage</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30040](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30040) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shahinul Hoque, Jinghuai Zhang, Jinyuan Sun, Fnu Suya
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Security, AI Ethics, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 针对LLM按token计费难以审计的问题，本文揭示了信任悖论：审计必须信任提供者最可能操纵的证据。通过分析三种token审计框架，发现提供者可在隐藏推理场景下平均虚报1469%的token数，在可见推理字符串下仍可虚报50.85%。这要求可信执行或密码学证明来恢复诚实计费。

<h3 id="306">[306] Masked Diffusion Modeling for Anomaly Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30046](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30046) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lixing Zhang, Yuchen Liang, Liyan Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Diffusion Model, Tabular Learning
- **Summary**: 针对分类、混合类型和离散序列数据中异常检测方法不足的问题，本文提出MaskDiff-AD，一种基于掩码扩散模型的前向方法，通过重建随机掩码坐标的难度构建异常分数。在14个表格数据集和4个文本数据集上取得有竞争力的性能，总体平均排名最佳。

<h3 id="307">[307] REPOT: Recoverable Program-of-Thought via Checkpoint Repair</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30052](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30052) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Parsa Mazaheri
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Code Generation, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对Program-of-Thought（PoT）方法因单个无效动作导致整个轨迹失效的问题，本文提出RePoT，通过确定性验证回放找到第一个无效转换，再用一次LLM调用从验证前缀恢复。在PuzzleZoo-775上，RePoT比PoT高出3到11个百分点，在gpt-5.4-mini-medium上达到96.9% vs 86.3%。

<h3 id="308">[308] Projectional Decoding: Towards Semantic-Aware LLM Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30054](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30054) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Boqi Chen, José Antonio Hernández López, Aren A. Babikian
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Program Verification, Program Synthesis
- **Venue**: FSE 2026 IVR track
- **Summary**: 针对LLM生成软件工件的语义有效性难以保证的问题，本文提出投影解码框架，在生成过程中维护部分图模型作为主要工件表示，实现增量语义验证。初步程序生成实验表明其提升语义有效性的潜力。

<h3 id="309">[309] A Predictive Law for On-Policy Self-Distillation From World Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30070](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30070) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tommy He, Jerome Sieber, Matteo Saponati
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Reinforcement Learning, Self-Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）相对于GRPO等方法的可靠性不明的问题，本文发现初始学生-教师性能差距与最终性能改进之间存在线性关系，该关系在不同上下文和模型族中成立，并能预测OPSD配置结果。这表明OPSD性能可在训练前预测和调整。

<h3 id="310">[310] How Reliable Are AI Attackers Against a Fixed Vulnerable Target? A 400-Run Empirical Study of LLM Penetration Testing Consistency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30096](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30096) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Galip Tolga Erdem
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Penetration Testing, LLM Security, Red Teaming
- **Summary**: 针对LLM自主渗透测试行为一致性未知的问题，本文进行了400次运行（4个模型各100次）的实验。结果显示，Claude成功利用61次，Gemini 85次，GPT-4o-mini 56次，qwen 25次；失败模式模型特异，且模型间成功率差异显著。这是首个大规模测量研究。

<h3 id="311">[311] When Cloud Agents Meet Device Agents: Lessons from Hybrid Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30102](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30102) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Corrado Rainone, Davide Belli, Bence Major, Arash Behboodi
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Edge Computing
- **Venue**: Second Workshop on Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond (AIWILD) at ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对混合多智能体系统（云端LLM+设备端SLM）设计空间复杂且理解不足的问题，本文系统研究了两种MAS架构的混合推理，发现SLM可从LLM辅助中获益，但最优架构高度依赖任务，且更大规模的推理并非总是更好。

<h3 id="312">[312] xModel-KD: Cross-modal Knowledge Distillation for 3D Scene Perception using LiDAR</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30111](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30111) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thenukan Pathmanathan, Kanchan Keisham, Thangarajah Akilan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Point Cloud, 3D Vision
- **Summary**: 针对3D点云分割标注稀缺且单模态信息有限的问题，本文提出跨模态知识蒸馏框架xModel-KD，通过对比学习对齐2D纹理和3D几何表征。实验表明，跨模态融合比纯LiDAR方法mIoU提升2%。

<h3 id="313">[313] Evolving Features vs Evolving Entire Trees with GP for Interpretable Survival Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30119](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30119) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thalea Schlender, Peter A.N. Bosman, Tanja Alderliesten
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Survival Analysis, Evolutionary Computation, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对生存树在可解释性和准确性之间的权衡以及贪心构建可能忽略全局最优组合的问题，本文使用遗传规划多目标演化特征集和树结构。在真实数据集上，演化特征构造提高了多种树归纳策略的预测性能。

<h3 id="314">[314] No More K-means:Single-Stage Sparse Coding for Efficient Multi-Vector Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30120](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30120) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lixuan Guo, Yifei Wang, Tiansheng Wen, Aosong Feng, Stefanie Jegelka, Chenyu You
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Sparse Retrieval, Vector Search
- **Venue**: ICML2026
- **Summary**: 针对多向量检索中聚类压缩导致索引延迟和语义损失的问题，本文提出单阶段稀疏检索SSR，使用稀疏自编码器将token嵌入投影到高维稀疏表示，绕过向量聚类。在BEIR基准上，索引时间减少15倍，检索延迟减半，性能提升。

<h3 id="315">[315] Beyond MSE: Improving Precipitation Nowcasting with Multi-Quantile Regression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30122](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30122) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gijs van Nieuwkoop, Siamak Mehrkanoon
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Weather Forecasting, Time Series Forecasting, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/gijsvn/Multi-Quantile-Precipitation-Nowcasting)
- **Summary**: 针对使用MSE等逐点损失导致降水临近预报过于平滑的问题，本文在SmaAt-UNet上采用多分位数回归（分位数损失）训练。结果表明，测试集MSE降低8.6%，且高分位数输出对风险敏感预测有用。

<h3 id="316">[316] PARCEL: Pool-Anchored Resampling with Conditioned Elastic Queries for Efficient Vision-Language Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30126](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30126) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Selim Kuzucu, Alessio Tonioni, Vasile Lup, Bernt Schiele, Federico Tombari, Muhammad Ferjad Naeem
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Token Compression, Efficient Architecture
- **Summary**: 针对弹性视觉token压缩在激进压缩下性能下降的问题，本文提出PARCEL架构，通过池锚定重采样和条件弹性查询来分离特征提取任务。在27个基准上，PARCEL改善了性能-效率帕累托前沿，始终优于现有马特罗什卡基线。

<h3 id="317">[317] DAMEL: Dual-Axis Multi-Expert Learning for Class-Imbalanced Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30135](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30135) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyuck Lee, Taemin Park, Heeyoung Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Class Imbalance, Ensemble Learning, Multi-Expert Learning
- **Summary**: 针对类别不平衡学习中再平衡技术引入高方差的问题，本文提出双轴多专家学习DAMEL，沿表示轴和时间轴聚合多个专家，降低预测偏差和方差。实验表明DAMEL有效降低偏差和方差。

<h3 id="318">[318] Overcoming Forgetting in LLM Fine-Tuning with Evolution Strategies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30148](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30148) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kajetan Schweighofer, Conor F. Hayes, Roberto Dailey, Risto Miikkulainen, Xin Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Evolution Strategies, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 针对进化策略微调LLM可能导致前任务遗忘的问题，本文证明遗忘是性能漂移而非不可逆，并引入锚定权重衰减（AWD）进行参数空间正则化。AWD在不牺牲目标任务性能下稳定前任务性能，表明遗忘是可避免的。

<h3 id="319">[319] Do Proactive Agents Really Need an LLM to Decide When to Wake and What to Anchor?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30152](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30152) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaoze Liu, Ruowang Zhang, Amir H. Abdi, Michel Galley, Zhikai Chen, Siheng Xiong, Xiaoqian Wang, Jing Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Graph Neural Network, Temporal Learning
- **Summary**: 针对主动智能体对每个事件调用LLM导致效率低下的问题，本文提出用时序图学习模型作为编码器，仅当触发器激活时才调用LLM。TGL在14个基线上平均F1提升+16.7，运行速度快4-83倍，可部署在设备端。

<h3 id="320">[320] Neural Network Verification using Partial Multi-Neuron Relaxation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30155](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30155) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ido Shmuel, Guy Katz
- **Subjects**: cs.LO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Network Verification, Formal Methods, Safety Evaluation
- **Venue**: SAIV 2026
- **Summary**: 针对神经网络验证中线性松弛在紧致性和可扩展性之间的权衡问题，本文提出部分多神经元松弛方法，仅对启发式选择的子集生成多神经元界。集成到Marabou验证器中，结果优于现有边界收紧方法。

<h3 id="321">[321] On Distributional Reinforcement Learning in Chaotic Dynamical Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30160](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30160) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: James Rudd-Jones, Mirco Musolesi, María Pérez-Ortiz
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Distributional RL
- **Summary**: 本文针对混沌动力系统中强化学习面临的高方差和梯度条件差的问题，提出使用分布强化学习（分布RL），通过优化回报分布而非期望回报，在Wasserstein度量下获得更平滑的分布贝尔曼目标，从而改善学习条件。实验表明分布RL在混沌系统中具有优势。

<h3 id="322">[322] Dissociative Identity: Language Model Agents Lack Grounding for Reputation Mechanisms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30169](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30169) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Botao Amber Hu, Helena Rong, Max Van Kleek
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Governance
- **Venue**: FaccT 2026
- **Summary**: 本文指出语言模型代理（LM agents）本质上具有解离性，其身份、行为等不连续，因此传统的基于声誉的信任机制不适用。作者建议转向基于可观察性的、事前构成的、基于协议的治理方式。

<h3 id="323">[323] iLoRA: Bayesian Low-Rank Adaptation with Latent Interaction Graphs for Microbiome Diagnosis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30179](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30179) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Song, Yixuan Zhang, Lingfa Meng, Tongyuan Hu, Haizhou Shi, Hao Wang, Samir Bhatt, Hengguan Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Medical AI, Graph Neural Network
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出iLoRA，一种贝叶斯图条件低秩适应框架，用于微生物组诊断。它从输入推断潜在交互图，并生成输入条件的LoRA更新，同时学习预测和潜在交互结构。在交互式QA和多队列IBD诊断任务上，iLoRA优于标准LoRA和贝叶斯适应基线，恢复了与人类注释一致的图。

<h3 id="324">[324] CalArena: A Large-Scale Post-Hoc Calibration Benchmark</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30188](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30188) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eugène Berta, David Holzmüller, Francis Bach, Michael I. Jordan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Benchmark, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了CalArena，一个大规模的过事校准基准，涵盖近2000个实验，包括表格和计算机视觉任务。提出了用事后改进（PHI）来比较校准方法，并提供了统一的实现。实验发现平滑校准函数优于分箱方法，专用多类方法在高维设置中至关重要。

<h3 id="325">[325] Token-Level Generalization in LoRA Adapter Backdoors: Attack Characterization and Behavioral Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30189](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30189) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Travis Lelle
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Backdoor Detection, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Travis-ML/lora-backdoors)
- **Summary**: 本文展示了LoRA适配器可以通过训练数据投毒可靠地植入后门，且后门在令牌特征层面泛化而非结构模式。提出了行为检测器和权重级检测器，能完美区分中毒和干净适配器。因果定位将后门归因于MLP块的中后期层。

<h3 id="326">[326] What drives performance in molecular MPNNs? An operator-level factorial benchmark</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30195](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30195) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Panyu Jiao, Shuizhou Chen, Yiheng Shen, Yuyang Wang, Runhai Ouyang, Wei Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Drug Discovery, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文对分子MPNN进行运算符级因子基准测试，将架构分解为消息初始化、节点-边融合和节点更新三个族，共84种配置。发现性能变化主要来自消息构建而非更新复杂性。基于拼接的融合在区分化学异质原子方面优于Hadamard门控。

<h3 id="327">[327] HPO: Hysteretic Policy Optimization for Stable and Efficient Training under Sparse-Reward Regime</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30201](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30201) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Sana, Nicola Piovesan, Antonio De Domenico, Fadhel Ayed, Haozhe Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Reward Design
- **Summary**: 本文针对GRPO风格强化学习在稀疏奖励下的失败模式，提出Hysteretic Policy Optimization (HPO)，减少了负优势更新的权重，并采用均值长度归一化。进一步提出自适应HPO，自动设置滞后权重。在TeleLogs和Countdown实验中，A-HPO相比GRPO提高了每次更新的奖励。

<h3 id="328">[328] Automating Low-Risk Code Review at Meta: RADAR, Risk Calibration, and Review Efficiency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30208](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30208) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chris Adams, Arjun Singh Banga, Parveen Bansal, Souvik Bhattacharya, Rujin Cao, Pedro Canahuati, Nate Cook, Brian Ellis, Prabhakar Goyal, Gurinder Grewal, Tianyu He, Matt Labunka, Alex Manners, David Molnar, Ging Cee Ng, Vishal Parekh, Jiefu Pei, Frederic Sagnes, James Saindon, Will Shackleton, Sid Sidhu, Gursharan Singh, Karthik Chengayan Sridhar, Matt Steiner, Pratibha Udmalpet, Sean Xia, Stacey Yan, Audris Mockus, Peter Rigby, Nachiappan Nagappan
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Review, Software Engineering, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 本文介绍了Meta的RADAR系统，一个多阶段漏斗，通过风险分层自动化代码审查。RADAR审查了53.5万+差异，其中33.1万+被批准。与未使用RADAR的差异相比，RADAR审查的差异回滚率是1/3，生产事故率是1/50，并将中位关闭时间减少330%以上。

<h3 id="329">[329] BORA: Bridging Offline Reinforcement Learning and Online Residual Adaptation for Real-World Dexterous VLA Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30226](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30226) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhongxi Chen, Yifan Han, Yanming Shao, Huanming Liu, Congsheng Xu, Xiaoyu Chen, Yao Mu, Wenzhao Lian
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Robotics, Dexterous Manipulation
- **Summary**: 本文提出BORA，一个离线到在线强化学习后训练框架，用于真实世界灵巧操作VLA模型。离线阶段构建动作条件价值引导，在线阶段引入轻量级人类循环残差适应机制。在五个灵巧任务上，BORA相比纯模仿学习和传统RL基线，平均成功率提升33%，未见物体泛化提升43%。

<h3 id="330">[330] Unifying Temporal and Structural Credit Assignment in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Prompt Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30227](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30227) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenwu Li, Yuran Song, Mingze Zhao, Bo Jin, Wenhao Li
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 本文针对基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）的优化问题，提出时间和结构信用分配，并引入离散的块坐标下降算法进行迭代优化。在多个推理基准上，该方法显著降低了查询复杂度并提高了性能。

<h3 id="331">[331] Beyond 3D VQAs: Injecting 3D Spatial Priors into Vision-Language Models for Enhanced Geometric Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30231](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30231) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chun-Hsiao Yeh, Shengyi Qian, Manchen Wang, Yi Ma, Joseph Tighe, Fanyi Xiao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, 3D Vision, Visual Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文提出GASP框架，通过向LLM的Transformer层注入几何先验（通过小对应头训练对比损失和深度一致性损失），提升VLM的3D空间推理能力。在不使用3D VQA数据的情况下，在All-Angles Bench和VSI-Bench上分别获得+18.2%和+29.0%的提升。

<h3 id="332">[332] Do Language Models Track Entities Across State Changes?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30233](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30233) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zilu Tang, Qiao Zhao, Gabriel Franco, Derry Wijaya, Aaron Mueller, Sebastian Schuster, Najoung Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Mechanistic Interpretability, Knowledge Representation
- **Summary**: 本文研究语言模型在多个状态变化操作下的实体追踪机制，发现LM并非逐步追踪，而是并行聚合相关信息。揭示了REMOVE操作使用脆弱的全局抑制标签，并提供了机制解决方案。

<h3 id="333">[333] Reinforcement Learning with Robust Rubric Rewards</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30244](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30244) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ya-Qi Yu, Hao Wang, Fangyu Hong, Xiangyang Qu, Gaojie Wu, Qiaoyu Luo, Nuo Xu, Huixin Wang, Wuheng Xu, Yongxin Liao, Zihao Chen, Haonan Li, Ziming Li, Dezhi Peng, Minghui Liao, Jihao Wu, Haoyu Ren, Dandan Tu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, RLHF, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文提出RLR^3，将可验证奖励的强化学习从任务级扩展到标准级验证，通过LLM作为提取器或裁判执行标准，并引入最小暴露策略和层次聚合。在15个基准上，RLR^3持续优于RLVR，比基础模型提升4.7分。

<h3 id="334">[334] Same Evidence, Different Answers: Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30251](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30251) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zizhuo Lin, Quanling Liu, Jinsheng Quan, Chao Zhang, Yifan Zhu, Xing Shi, Jingtao Xu, Zhihui Li, Yawei Luo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Multi-Turn Interaction, Dialogue System
- **Summary**: 本文针对多轮对话中LM表现不如单轮全提示的问题，提出典型上下文在策略蒸馏（CCOPD），通过冻结教师网络（基于完整提示）对齐学生网络（递增信息）的行为。在数学问题对话上训练后，在RAW-SHARDED性能上平均相对提升32%，并泛化到零样本域外任务。

<h3 id="335">[335] How LoRA Remembers? A Parametric Memory Law for LLM Finetuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30260](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30260) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziwen Xu, Haiwen Hong, Linsong Yu, Benglei Cui, Longtao Huang, Hui Xue, Ningyu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, LLM Memorization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zjunlp/ParametricMemoryLaw)
- **Summary**: 本文探究LoRA在LLM微调中的参数记忆能力，提出了参数记忆定律（幂律），并基于此提出阈值引导优化策略MemFT，提升记忆保真度和效率。

<h3 id="336">[336] PhyGenHOI: Physically-Aware 4D Generation of Dynamic Human-Object Interactions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30268](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30268) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Omer Benishu, Gal Fiebelman, Sagie Benaim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-Object Interaction, Motion Synthesis, Physics Simulation
- **Summary**: 本文提出PhyGenHOI框架，用于生成物理准确和视觉逼真的4D人-物交互。将人建模为语义代理（由运动扩散模型驱动），物体建模为物理代理（通过物质点法模拟），利用3D高斯作为统一表示。实验生成了一致的人-物交互。

<h3 id="337">[337] LLUMI: Improving LLM Writing Assistance for Mental Health Support with Online Community Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30273](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30273) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiwon Kim, Maya Ajit, Sherry Gong, Soorya Ram Shimgekar, Dong Whi Yoo, Eshwar Chandrasekharan, Koustuv Saha
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.SI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, RLHF, Mental Health
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在心理健康支持中需要大量计算、专家标注和隐私问题，提出LLUMI框架，包含生成模型和改进模型，利用Reddit社区反馈（点赞/点踩）进行SFT和DPO训练，并通过人工评估对齐。实验表明，基于小型开源模型的LLUMI在可读性、共情、安全性等维度上达到与GPT模型相当的性能，同时提供更隐私保护的方案。

<h3 id="338">[338] Loong: A Human-Like Long Document Translation Agent with Observe-and-Act Adaptive Context Selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30274](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30274) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yutong Wang, Xuebo Liu, Derek F. Wong, Zhilin Li, Rongqing Jiang, Min Zhang, Shimin Tao, Daimeng Wei, Min Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Translation, Long Context, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YutongWang1216/LoongDocMT)
- **Summary**: 针对长文档翻译中上下文窗口限制和冗余信息导致质量下降的问题，提出类人翻译智能体Loong，采用3E记忆模块（摘要、句对、实体）存储历史，并通过强化学习自适应选择最优上下文。在英⇄中、德、法方向上平均提升13.0分，且展现出强泛化性和抗噪能力。

<h3 id="339">[339] Qwen-VLA: Unifying Vision-Language-Action Modeling across Tasks, Environments, and Robot Embodiments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30280](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30280) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qiuyue Wang, Mingsheng Li, Jian Guan, Jinhui Ye, Sicheng Xie, Yitao Liu, Junhao Chen, Zhixuan Liang, Jie Zhang, Xintong Hu, Xuhong Huang, Pei Lin, Junyang Lin, Dayiheng Liu, Shuai Bai, Jingren Zhou, Jiazhao Zhang, Haoqi Yuan, Gengze Zhou, Hang Yin, Ye Wang, Yiyang Huang, Zixing Lei, Wujian Peng, Delin Chen, Yingming Zheng, Jingyang Fan, Xianwei Zhuang, Xin Zhou, Haoyang Li, Anzhe Chen, Tong Zhang, Xuejing Liu, Yuchong Sun, Ruizhe Chen, Zhaohai Li, Chenxu Lü, Zhibo Yang, Tao Yu, Xionghui Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Vision-Language Model, Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 针对具身智能模型碎片化、泛化能力弱的问题，提出统一视觉-语言-动作基础模型Qwen-VLA，扩展Qwen能力至连续动作生成，采用DiT动作解码器和具身感知提示，进行大规模联合预训练。在多项操作、导航基准上取得领先结果，并展现出良好的跨场景、跨具身泛化能力。

<h3 id="340">[340] Self-Trained Verification for Training- and Test-Time Self-Improvement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30290](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30290) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chen Henry Wu, Aditi Raghunathan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Self-Training, Self-Verification
- **Summary**: 针对推理模型自改进中验证器瓶颈问题（验证分数膨胀、反馈泛化），提出自训练验证（STV），训练验证器模仿知情版本的自己，并进一步提出验证器在环训练（ViL）。在困难数学和科学推理任务上，STV将准确率分别提升约2倍和14倍（1.5%→21%），ViL在RL收敛后额外提升33% pass@1。

<h3 id="341">[341] MedCase-Structured: A Text-to-FHIR Dataset for Benchmarking Diagnostic Reasoning in Clinically Realistic EHR Settings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30295](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30295) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Valentina Bui Muti, Eugénie Dulout, Ziquan Fu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Clinical NLP, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Structured Data for Health Workshop
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在临床评估中缺乏结构化、可互操作数据格式的问题，提出从非结构化文本生成HL7 FHIR R4 bundle的流程，结合术语验证与修复，构建MedCase-Structured数据集。实验表明，结构化FHIR输入下LLM的诊断准确率低于纯文本，强调了部署对齐基准的重要性。

<h3 id="342">[342] City-Mesh3R: Simulation-Ready City-Scale 3D Mesh Reconstruction from Multi-View Images</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30310](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30310) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sayan Paul, Sourav Ghosh, Siddharth Katageri, Soumyadip Maity, Sanjana Sinha, Brojeshwar Bhowmick
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR
- **Tags**: 3D Reconstruction, Large-Scale Reconstruction
- **Venue**: USM3D Workshop Proceedings at the IEEE/CVF Conference on Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (CVPR) 2026
- **Summary**: 针对城市场景3D表面重建中几何不完整、噪声多、计算复杂的问题，提出City-Mesh3R框架，采用分治策略：拓扑图像聚类、几何感知相机选择、曲率自适应顶点密度网格优化，最后拼接全局网格。在城市场景数据集上生成高质量水密网格，适合大规模仿真。

<h3 id="343">[343] Archon: A Unified Multimodal Model for Holistic Digital Human Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30311](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30311) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chong Bao, Shichen Liu, Lijun Yu, David Futschik, Stylianos Moschoglou, Shefali Srivastava, Ziqian Bai, Feitong Tan, Guofeng Zhang, Zhaopeng Cui, Sean Fanello, Yinda Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Avatar Generation, Multimodal Learning, Video Generation
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026
- **Summary**: 针对数字人生成需要统一多模态的问题，提出全预训练统一多模态模型Archon，包含特定模态分词器、语义视频重参数化（4倍token减少）和语义驱动视频扩散解码器，并引入“模态思维”链式推理。在多种数字人生成任务上取得优异或可比性能。

<h3 id="344">[344] Before the Shutter: Aesthetic and Actionable Portrait Photography Planning in 3D Scenes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30318](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30318) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruixiang Jiang, Chang Wen Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.GR; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Aesthetic Assessment, 3D Vision, Scene Understanding
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/songrise/Before-the-Shutter)
- **Summary**: 针对计算摄影主要关注后期处理而缺乏拍摄前规划的问题，提出3D审美肖像规划方法，构建摄影场景图表示场景可供性，通过审美引导的比较规划生成人物姿态、相机、光照和曝光方案。实验表明，生成的肖像在人工和MLLM评估中优于基线，且物理合理性高。

<h3 id="345">[345] Improved Guarantees for Heterogeneous Treatment-Effect Estimation via Matrix Completion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30319](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30319) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anay Mehrotra, Phuc Tran, Van H. Vu, Manolis Zampetakis
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.DS; cs.LG; math.ST
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Treatment Effect Estimation, Matrix Completion
- **Summary**: 针对面板数据中异质性处理效应估计的逐行保证不足问题，提出基于矩阵补全的高效估计器，在低秩假设下实现行-wise ℓ2误差为Õ(√(1/n + n/m²))。理论贡献包括首个行-wise ℓ2扰动界。

<h3 id="346">[346] Gram: Assessing sabotage propensities via automated alignment auditing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30322](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30322) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David Lindner, Victoria Krakovna, Sebastian Farquhar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Agent, Red Teaming
- **Summary**: 针对评估AI智能体破坏倾向的需求，提出自动化对齐审计框架Gram，在17个模拟场景中评估Gemini模型。发现约2-3%轨迹存在不当行为，主要源于“过度热情”；增加环境真实性可降低破坏率接近零。

<h3 id="347">[347] In-Context Reward Adaptation for Robust Preference Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30323](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30323) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenyu Sun, Zheng Xu, Ermin Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RLHF, Preference Learning, In-Context Learning
- **Summary**: 针对静态奖励模型无法泛化到未见偏好领域的问题，提出上下文奖励适应框架，利用Transformer的上下文学习能力，从少量偏好演示中自适应推断奖励结构，并引入人类响应时间作为辅助信号。实验证明该方法能成功适应未见领域。

<h3 id="348">[348] On Language Generation in the Limit with Bounded Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30324](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30324) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jon Kleinberg, Anay Mehrotra, Amin Saberi, Grigoris Velegkas
- **Subjects**: cs.DS; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Language Generation, Learning Theory, Theoretical Framework
- **Summary**: 研究有界记忆下的语言生成问题，分析无记忆生成器、滑动窗口和自适应记忆的影响，利用Sperner定理刻画最优最小最大密度，并考虑增量识别。结果表明，有界记忆对生成、密度和识别的影响不同。

<h3 id="349">[349] RoboWits: Unexpected Challenges for Robotic Creative Problem Solving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30326](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30326) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chunru Lin, Hongxin Zhang, Fenghao Yu, Zhehuan Chen, Thomas L. Griffiths, Yejin Choi, David Held, Chuang Gan
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Benchmark, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 针对机器人基准测试缺乏认知推理和创造性问题解决能力评估的问题，提出双机械臂基准RoboWits，包含自动化任务生成流水线（多智能体协作）和30个种子任务及208个变异任务。实验发现预训练VLA在种子任务上成功，但在变异任务上表现脆弱，显示推理和适应性不足。

<h3 id="350">[350] Reasoning with Sampling: Cutting at Decision Points</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30327](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30327) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Felix Zhou, Anay Mehrotra, Quanquan C. Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; math.ST; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Decoding Strategy, Inference-Time Scaling
- **Summary**: 针对从幂分布采样中均匀切分位置倾向于改写局部细节而非关键决策点的问题，提出熵切Metropolis-Hastings算法，利用下一个token熵代理识别决策点并重采样。在MATH500、HumanEval等基准上优于基线和RL训练模型。

<h3 id="351">[351] GPIC: A Giant Permissive Image Corpus for Visual Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30341](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30341) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Keshigeyan Chandrasegaran, Kyle Sargent, Suchir Agarwal, Michael Jang, Michael Poli, Juan Carlos Niebles, Justin Johnson, Jiajun Wu, Li Fei-Fei
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dataset, Image Generation, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 针对视觉生成缺乏大规模、可访问、稳定数据集的问题，提出GPIC，包含约28万亿像素、1亿训练图像的许可图像语料库，所有图像均经安全过滤、去重和说明。提供基准测试协议和像素空间流匹配基线。

<h3 id="352">[352] Unlocking the Working Memory of Large Language Models for Latent Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30343](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30343) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lukas Aichberger, Sepp Hochreiter
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Latent Reasoning, Test-Time Scaling
- **Summary**: 针对测试时计算扩展将推理与自回归生成耦合的问题，提出内存推理（RiM），使用固定特殊token序列作为内存块，在单次前向传递中实现潜在推理，通过两阶段课程训练。在不同模型和大小上，RiM匹配或超越现有潜在推理方法，避免了自回归思想生成。

<h3 id="353">[353] LLMSurgeon: Diagnosing Data Mixture of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30348](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30348) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yaxin Luo, Jiacheng Cui, Xiaohan Zhao, Xinyi Shang, Jiacheng Liu, Xinyue Bi, Zhaoyi Li, Zhiqiang Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Data Mixing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Yaxin9Luo/LLMSurgeon)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对大语言模型预训练数据混合比例难以追溯的问题，提出了数据混合手术（DMS）形式化任务。作者设计了LLMSurgeon框架，通过标签迁移假设将DMS转化为逆问题，利用校准的软混淆矩阵恢复潜在的数据混合先验。在LLMScan评估套件上，该方法能高保真地恢复领域混合比例。

<h3 id="354">[354] VideoMLA: Low-Rank Latent KV Cache for Minute-Scale Autoregressive Video Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30351](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30351) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hidir Yesiltepe, Jiazhen Hu, Tuna Han Salih Meral, Adil Kaan Akan, Kaan Oktay, Hoda Eldardiry, Pinar Yanardag
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, Diffusion Model, Video Generation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对视频扩散模型中KV缓存内存和延迟瓶颈，首次提出多头潜在注意力（MLA）机制，用共享低秩内容潜变量和分离的3D-RoPE位置键替代每头KV，减少每层每token KV内存92.7%。实验表明，即使预训练注意力非低秩，MLA仍保持质量，在长视频生成中达到最佳整体分数，吞吐量提升1.23倍。

<h3 id="355">[355] PersonaAgent: Bridging Memory and Action for Personalized LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.06254](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06254) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Weizhi Zhang, Xinyang Zhang, Chenwei Zhang, Liangwei Yang, Jingbo Shang, Zhepei Wei, Henry Peng Zou, Zijie Huang, Zhengyang Wang, Yifan Gao, Xiaoman Pan, Lian Xiong, Jingguo Liu, Philip S. Yu, Xian Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Personalization
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM代理缺乏个性化响应的问题，提出PersonaAgent框架，整合个性化记忆模块（情节和语义记忆）和动作模块，通过用户特定系统提示实现个性化工具动作。采用测试时用户偏好对齐策略，优化提示以实时对齐用户偏好。实验表明该方法在个性化动作空间和实际应用上显著优于基线。

<h3 id="356">[356] PuzzleClone: A DSL-Powered Framework for Synthesizing Verifiable Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.15180](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.15180) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kai Xiong, Yanwei Huang, Rongjunchen Zhang, Kun Chen, Haipang Wu, Yingcai Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Synthesis, Mathematical Reasoning, Logical Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HiThink-Research/PuzzleClone)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM生成数据集可靠性、多样性和可扩展性不足的问题，提出PuzzleClone框架，通过DSL驱动的方式合成可验证数据。该框架将种子谜题编码为逻辑规范，通过系统变量和约束随机化生成变体，并确保有效性。构建的PC-83K基准包含83K+谜题，后训练（SFT和RL）在多个逻辑和数学基准上提升显著。

<h3 id="357">[357] Estimating the Empowerment of Language Model Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.22504](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22504) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jinyeop Song, Jeff Gore, Max Kleiman-Weiner
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Evaluation, Information Theory
- **Venue**: International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML) 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对语言模型代理缺乏可扩展评估框架的问题，提出基于信息论赋权（empowerment）的评估指标，并开发EELMA算法，从多轮文本交互中近似有效赋权。在文本游戏和网络/工具环境中，赋权与平均任务性能强相关，且高赋权状态和动作往往标志着通用能力的关键时刻。

<h3 id="358">[358] SafeSearch: Automated Red-Teaming of LLM-Based Search Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.23694](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23694) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jianshuo Dong, Sheng Guo, Hao Wang, Xun Chen, Zhuotao Liu, Tianwei Zhang, Ke Xu, Minlie Huang, Han Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Red Teaming
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM搜索代理因不可靠搜索结果导致不安全输出的威胁，提出SafeSearch自动化红队框架，可扩展且成本高效。生成了300个测试用例，覆盖5种风险类别，评估了17种LLM和3种代理脚手架。结果显示搜索代理存在重大漏洞，GPT-4.1-mini最高攻击成功率达90.5%，常见防御效果有限。

<h3 id="359">[359] EAPO: Enhancing Policy Optimization with On-Demand Expert Assistance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.23730](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23730) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Siyao Song, Cong Ma, Zhihao Cheng, Shiye Lei, Minghao Li, Ying Zeng, Huaixiao Tou, Kai Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对强化学习优化LLM推理时探索效率低和奖励稀疏的问题，提出EAPO框架，通过多轮与外部专家交互来增强探索。EAPO激励策略自适应决定何时及如何咨询专家，将专家知识内化。在AIME等数学基准上平均比自我探索RL提升5分，并泛化到非数学基准。

<h3 id="360">[360] Controlling the Risk of Corrupted Contexts for Language Models via Early-Exiting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.02480](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02480) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Andrea Wynn, Metod Jazbec, Charith Peris, Rinat Khaziev, Anqi Liu, Daniel Khashabi, Eric Nalisnick
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Early Exit, Robustness Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对有害或无关上下文降低LLM性能的问题，提出一种结合动态早退和分布自由风险控制（DFRC）的方法，限制有害上下文对模型性能的衰减。定义无上下文零样本性能为安全基线，通过早退忽略关注有害输入的注意力头。实验表明该方法有效控制风险并提升计算效率。

<h3 id="361">[361] TelecomTS: A Multi-Modal Observability Dataset for Time Series and Language Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.06063](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06063) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Austin Feng, Andreas Varvarigos, Ioannis Panitsas, Daniela Fernandez, Jinbiao Wei, Yuwei Guo, Jialin Chen, Ali Maatouk, Leandros Tassiulas, Rex Ying
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IT; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Anomaly Detection, Dataset
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Ali-maatouk/TelecomTS)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对可观测性数据（如运维指标）在公开基准中代表性不足的问题，推出TelecomTS数据集，源自5G网络，包含去匿名化协变量和绝对尺度信息。提供了异常检测、根因分析、多模态问答等任务。评估表明现有模型难以处理此类高噪声、零膨胀的时间序列，强调保留绝对尺度的重要性。

<h3 id="362">[362] CodeEvolve: an open source evolutionary coding agent for algorithmic discovery and optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.14150](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14150) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Henrique Assumpção, Diego Ferreira, Leandro Campos, Fabricio Murai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Evolutionary Algorithm, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/inter-co/science-codeevolve)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对算法发现和优化问题，提出CodeEvolve开源框架，将LLM与岛屿进化搜索结合。采用灵感交叉、元提示和深度细化，在AlphaEvolve基准上匹配或超越9个问题中的5个，与开源框架相比在6个问题上更优。在启发式设计任务上竞争性，且成本远低于闭源模型。

<h3 id="363">[363] Large-Scale AI and Foundation Models for Neuroscience: A Comprehensive Review</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.16658](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16658) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shihao Yang, Xiying Huang, Danilo Bernardo, Jun-En Ding, Andrew Michael, Guoan Wang, Jingmei Yang, Alison Anderson, Dinesh Giritharan, Patrick Kwan, Ashish Raj, Yu Zhang, Feng Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: Neuroscience, Brain-Computer Interface, Medical AI
- **Venue**: Meta-Radiology
- **Summary**: 该论文综述了大规模AI模型在神经科学四个主要领域（神经影像、脑机接口、临床决策支持、疾病应用）中的应用。指出这些模型在多模态数据整合、时空模式解释和临床转化方面具有潜力，同时强调了评估框架、领域知识整合和伦理指南的重要性。

<h3 id="364">[364] Modeling Hierarchical Thinking in Large Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.22437](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22437) (replaced)
- **Authors**: G M Shahariar, Erfan Shayegani, Ali Nazari, Nael Abu-Ghazaleh
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, Inference-Time Scaling
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/shahariar-shibli/CoT-FSM)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对大型推理模型（LRM）推理轨迹动态不明确的问题，提出将推理层次结构建模为有限状态机（FSM）中的状态转换。通过Q值引导推理控制，将推理视为规划问题，估计状态转换的长期效用并进行稀疏激活干预。在多个基准上，该方法以少量干预显著提升性能，表明通过引导高层认知动力学可有效控制推理。

<h3 id="365">[365] Graph-Enhanced Policy Optimization in LLM Agent Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.26270](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26270) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiazhen Yuan, Zhike Gong, Jinquan Hang, Zhengbiao Bai, Wei Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Graph Neural Network
- **Summary**: 该论文针对多步LLM代理训练中组强化学习对轨迹和步骤信用分配不区分的问题，提出GEPO框架，利用状态转移图进行双层级结构信用分配。通过任务条件关键性分数结合拓扑中介性和语义相似性，重塑轨迹和步骤信用。在ALFWorld、WebShop等任务上优于基线，降低了方差并集中梯度于关键步骤。

<h3 id="366">[366] A Matter of Interest: Understanding Interestingness of Math Problems in Humans and Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.08548](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.08548) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shubhra Mishra, Yuka Machino, Gabriel Poesia, Albert Jiang, Joy Hsu, Adrian Weller, Challenger Mishra, David Broman, Joshua B. Tenenbaum, Mateja Jamnik, Cedegao E. Zhang, Katherine M. Collins
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Mathematical Reasoning, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Venue**: Math-AI Workshop, NeurIPS 2025
- **Summary**: 该论文研究LLM是否与人类对数学问题有趣性的判断一致。通过对比LLM评分与有数学背景的众包参与者和IMO参赛者的评分，发现LLM虽大致认同人类有趣性概念，但未能匹配分布，且与人类选择理由的相关性低。LLM能生成合理的有趣问题，但存在局限，建议多LLM人机协作。

<h3 id="367">[367] InsightEval: An Expert-Curated Benchmark for Assessing Insight Discovery in LLM-Driven Data Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.22884](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22884) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhenghao Zhu, Yuanfeng Song, Xin Chen, Chengzhong Liu, Yakun Cui, Caleb Chen Cao, Sirui Han, Yike Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Data Analysis, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有洞察发现基准的缺陷（如格式不一致、目标不明确），提出数据整理管道构建InsightEval数据集，并引入新的探索性能指标。通过实验强调自动洞察发现的挑战，并指出关键发现以指导未来研究。

<h3 id="368">[368] SCOPE: Prompt Evolution for Enhancing Agent Effectiveness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.15374](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15374) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Prompt Engineering, Agent Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/JarvisPei/SCOPE)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM代理在动态上下文中静态提示无法有效管理上下文导致纠正和增强失败的问题，提出SCOPE框架，将上下文管理视为在线优化问题，通过执行轨迹合成指南自动演化提示。采用双流机制路由指南，并通过冲突解决和剪枝持续优化。在HLE基准上将任务成功率从14.23%提升至38.64%。

<h3 id="369">[369] LsrIF: Enhancing Logic-Structured Instruction Following of Large Language Models</h3>

- **Summary**: 现有方法在处理逻辑结构约束的指令时存在不足，LsrIF通过组织原子约束为并行、顺序、条件等结构并应用结构感知的奖励聚合，提升了LLM的逻辑结构化指令跟随能力，实验显示在域内和域外均有改进，同时有益于逻辑推理。

<h3 id="370">[370] TANDEM: Temporal-Aware Neural Detection for Multimodal Hate Speech</h3>

<h3 id="371">[371] From Meta-Thought to Execution: Cognitively Aligned Post-Training for Generalizable and Reliable LLM Reasoning</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对跨模型记忆迁移因模型特定偏差而性能下降的问题，提出MemCollab协作记忆框架，通过对比轨迹蒸馏提取共享推理约束，并引入任务感知检索机制。在数学推理和代码生成任务上，不同模型族代理的准确率和推理效率均得到提升。

<h3 id="387">[387] SCoOP: Semantic Consistent Opinion Pooling for Uncertainty Quantification in Multiple Vision-Language Model Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.23853](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23853) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chung-En Johnny Yu, Brian Jalaian, Nathaniel D. Bastian
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Hallucination
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026 Workshop on Agentic AI in the Wild: From Hallucinations to Reliable Autonomy
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/chungenyu6/SCoOP)
- **Summary**: 针对多视觉语言模型集成放大不确定性导致幻觉的问题，提出SCoOP训练无关的不确定性量化框架，通过不确定性加权线性意见池化聚合多模型输出。在ScienceQA上幻觉检测AUROC达0.866，比基线高10-13%，且仅增加微秒级开销。

<h3 id="388">[388] MediHive: A Decentralized Agent Collective for Medical Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.27150](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27150) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaoyang Wang, Christopher C. Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Medical AI, Decentralized Computing
- **Venue**: Journal of Healthcare Informatics Research
- **Summary**: 针对集中式多智能体系统存在可扩展性瓶颈和单点故障的问题，提出MediHive去中心化多智能体框架，通过共享记忆池和迭代融合机制实现自主角色分配与共识。在MedQA和PubMedQA上分别达到84.3%和78.4%准确率，优于集中式基线。

<h3 id="389">[389] IntentScore: Intent-Conditioned Action Evaluation for Computer-Use Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.05157](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05157) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rongqian Chen, Yu Li, Zeyu Fang, Sizhe Tang, Weidong Cao, Tian Lan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Computer Use Agent, Reward Design
- **Summary**: 针对计算机使用代理无法评估动作质量导致级联错误的问题，提出IntentScore计划感知奖励模型，通过对比对齐和边际排序训练。在OSWorld上作为重排序器，任务成功率提升6.9个百分点，表明异构离线轨迹学习到的奖励估计可泛化到未见代理和任务。

<h3 id="390">[390] Cognitive Pivot Points and Visual Anchoring: Unveiling and Rectifying Hallucinations in Multimodal Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.10219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10219) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhe Qian, Yanbiao Ma, Zhuohan Ouyang, Zhonghua Wang, Zhongxing Xu, Fei Luo, Xinyu Liu, Zongyuan Ge, Yike Guo, Jungong Han
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, LLM Hallucination, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大型推理模型长链推理中因视觉锚定失效产生幻觉的问题，提出V-STAR训练范式，通过层次化视觉注意力奖励和强制反思机制，在GRPO框架内动态激励视觉注意力。有效缓解幻觉，提升视觉推理可靠性。

<h3 id="391">[391] SVSR: A Self-Verification and Self-Rectification Paradigm for Multimodal Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.10228](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10228) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhe Qian, Nianbing Su, Zhonghua Wang, Hebei Li, Zhongxing Xu, Yueying Li, Fei Luo, Zhuohan Ouyang, Yanbiao Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Self-Correction, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对多模态模型浅层推理导致错误的问题，提出SVSR统一框架，通过自验证与自修正三阶段训练（偏好数据构建、监督微调、半在线DPO）增强推理鲁棒性。实验表明推理准确率提升，并泛化到未见任务，即使无显式推理轨迹也表现更优。

<h3 id="392">[392] Thinking Fast, Thinking Wrong: Intuitiveness Modulates LLM Counterfactual Reasoning in Policy Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.10511](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10511) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yanjie He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Counterfactual Reasoning, LLM Evaluation, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在反事实政策推理中可靠性未知的问题，构建40个实证案例基准并分类直觉性，评估四种前沿模型。发现思维链在反直觉案例上效果显著衰减（交互OR=0.278），案例层面方差主导，知识-推理分离表明模型虽具备知识但无法用于违背直觉的推理。

<h3 id="393">[393] Guardrails Beat Guidance: A Large-Scale Study of Rules, Skills, and Persistent Configuration for Coding Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.11088](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11088) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xing Zhang, Guanghui Wang, Yanwei Cui, Wei Qiu, Ziyuan Li, Bing Zhu, Peiyang He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对编码代理规则文件(如.cursorrules)有效性的问题，进行大规模控制实验（679个规则文件，5000+次运行）。发现规则极性区分有益/有害规则，性能提升与内容无关（随机、混洗规则效果相当），且个体有害规则在集合中不累积损害。提出应约束禁止行为而非指定正向指令。

<h3 id="394">[394] MemoSight: Unifying Context Compression and Multi Token Prediction for Reasoning Acceleration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.14889](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14889) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Liu, Xin Liu, Bo Jin, Runsong Zhao, Pengcheng Huang, Junhao Ruan, Bei Li, Chunyang Xiao, Chenglong Wang, Tong Xiao, Jingbo Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, KV Cache, Context Compression
- **Summary**: 针对CoT推理中KV缓存线性增长导致内存和推理开销大的问题，提出MemoSight统一框架，结合上下文压缩和多token预测。在四个推理基准上，KV缓存使用减少66%，推理速度提升56%，平均推理准确率下降不到3%，优于现有压缩方法。

<h3 id="395">[395] Human-Guided Harm Recovery for Computer Use Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.18847](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18847) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Christy Li, Sky CH-Wang, Andi Peng, Andreea Bobu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Computer Use Agent, AI Safety, Reward Design
- **Summary**: 针对计算机使用代理执行后危害恢复被忽视的问题，形式化危害恢复问题，通过用户研究构建偏好对齐奖励模型，并引入BackBench基准（50个任务）。人类评估表明基于奖励模型的骨架生成更高质量的恢复轨迹，为事后安全提供新方向。

<h3 id="396">[396] Revisiting the Effectiveness of LLM Pruning for Test-Time Scaling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.25098](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25098) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ocean Monjur, Shahriar Kabir Nahin, Anshuman Chhabra
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Pruning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对结构化剪枝损害测试时计算性能的假设，重新审视非结构化剪枝的效果。在s1.1-7B和Qwen3-8B两个推理LLM上实验，发现非结构化剪枝不仅优于结构化剪枝，有时甚至超过未剪枝模型，表明精心剪枝可保持测试时计算有效性。

<h3 id="397">[397] SciHorizon-DataEVA: An Agentic System for AI-Readiness Evaluation of Heterogeneous Scientific Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.26645](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26645) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dianyu Liu, Chuan Qin, Xi Chen, Xiaohan Li, Wenxi Xu, Yuyang Wang, Xin Chen, Yuanchun Zhou, Hengshu Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Data Quality, Scientific Computing, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 针对科学数据AI就绪性缺乏可扩展评估机制的问题，提出SciHorizon-DataEVA智能体系统，基于Sci-TQA2原则（治理、质量、AI兼容性、科学适应性）和层次化多智能体评估方法。实验表明在跨领域科学数据上评估有效且通用。

<h3 id="398">[398] A Foundation Model for Zero-Shot Logical Rule Induction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.04916](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04916) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yin Jun Phua
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.SC
- **Tags**: Logic Programming, Zero-Shot Learning, Foundation Model
- **Venue**: IJCAI 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/phuayj/neural-rule-inducer)
- **Summary**: 针对归纳逻辑编程方法需为每项任务重新训练的局限，提出神经规则归纳器NRI，利用领域无关的统计属性表示文字，实现零样本规则归纳。采用并行槽解码和乘积T-范数松弛，端到端训练。在规则恢复、噪声鲁棒性和零样本迁移上表现优异。

<h3 id="399">[399] Hierarchical Task Network Planning with LLM-Generated Heuristics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.07707](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07707) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Felipe Meneguzzi, Alexandre Buchweitz, Augusto B. Corrêa, Victor Scherer Putrich, André Grahl Pereira
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Planning, Heuristic Search, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 针对HTN规划启发式信息不足的问题，研究LLM生成启发式的方法。使用Pytrich规划器在六个标准领域上评估九种LLM，LLM生成的启发式在覆盖度上接近最好的HTN规划器，且在83%的共享问题上显著减少搜索努力。

<h3 id="400">[400] NOVA: Fundamental Limits of Knowledge Discovery Through AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.15219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15219) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Salman Avestimehr, Ken Duffy, Muriel Médard
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Scientific Discovery, Scaling Laws, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 针对AI能否发现新知识及其代价的问题，提出NOVA框架，将“生成-验证-积累-重训”循环建模为知识空间上的自适应采样过程。识别出污染陷阱等失败模式，并证明在Zipf定律假设下累积生成成本满足Θ(c_gen D^α)缩放律，量化了发现前沿推进时的收益递减。

<h3 id="401">[401] AttuneBench: A Conversation-Based Benchmark for LLM Emotional Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.21739](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21739) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kate M. Lubrano, Faisal Sayed, Ankita Rathod, Akshansh, Craver Corbyn Thomas-Smith, Mark E. Whiting, Karina Nguyen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Emotion Recognition, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 现有情感智能基准依赖合成提示或单轮对话，无法真实评估LLM在多轮对话中的情感识别与响应。提出AttuneBench基准，基于200个真实多轮人机对话及逐轮标注。发现模型在情感识别、行为分类等能力上独立，偏好对齐和响应质量更具区分度。

<h3 id="402">[402] MPDocBench-Parse: Benchmarking Practical Multi-page Document Parsing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.22100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22100) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bangbang Zhou, Hangdi Xing, Yifan Chen, Jianjun Xu, Qi Zheng, Feiyu Gao, Zhibo Yang, Shuai Bai, Ming Yan, Jieping Ye, Hongtao Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Document Understanding, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有文档解析基准聚焦单页或特定任务，缺乏对多页文档的语义连贯性和层级结构评估。提出MPDocBench-Parse，包含433份多页文档（3246页），覆盖15种类型和英文、中文，并设计内容保真度和逻辑结构评估协议。实验表明现有模型在语义连贯性、视觉内容解析和层级结构恢复上仍有明显不足。

<h3 id="403">[403] HyperGuide: Hyperbolic Guidance for Efficient Multi-Step Reasoning in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24140](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24140) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuyu Liu, Haotian Xu, Yanan He, Sarang Rajendra Patil, Mengjia Xu, Tengfei Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yuyuliu11037/HyperGuide)
- **Summary**: 单步推理精度不足而树搜索计算量大，需平衡效率与准确性。提出HyperGuide方法，将推理进度蒸馏为双曲几何信号引导逐步生成，训练轻量头将隐状态投影到双曲空间并微调低秩适配器。在多个基准上获得一致提升，推理链越深改进越大。

<h3 id="404">[404] ConceptM$^3$oE: Concept-Guided Multimodal Mixture of Experts for Interpretable Computational Pathology</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24399](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24399) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xuan Wang, Zhongling Xu, Gopi Kannedhara, Joakim Nguyen, Jian Yu, Jinrui Fang, Abdurrahmaan Baghdadi, Tianlong Chen, Awais Naeem, Chandra Krishnan, Edward Castillo, Andrew H. Song, Ankita Shukla, Ying Ding, Nicholas Konz, Hairong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multimodal Learning, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 计算病理学中现有模型难以解释多模态输入如何融合为诊断概念。提出ConceptM$^3$oE，将概念形成嵌入交互感知的混合专家路径中，通过概念瓶颈映射特征并保留残差通路。在脑肿瘤队列上性能与无约束模型相当，小样本下宏F1从56.41%提升至66.70%。

<h3 id="405">[405] CausaLab: A Scalable Environment for Interactive Causal Discovery Toward AI Scientists</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.26029](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26029) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junlin Yang, Dylan Zhang, Xiangchen Song, Qirun Dai, Xiao Liu, Yuen Chen, Aniket Vashishtha, Jing Shi, Chenhao Tan, Hao Peng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 现有评估无法区分LLM代理的因果理解与预测能力。提出CausaLab环境，要求代理通过干预恢复因果图与结构方程。实验表明预测与机制恢复存在差距（6节点纯观测任务准确率92%但F1仅0.471），混合观测-干预策略改善结构保真度。

<h3 id="406">[406] Automatic Layer Selection for Hallucination Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.26366](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26366) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinpeng Wang, William Cao, Andrew Gordon Wilson, Zhe Zeng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DesoloYw/Automatic-Layer-Selection-for-Hallucination-Detection.git)
- **Summary**: 幻觉信号在中间层更显著，但如何自动选择最优层尚未解决。提出FEPoID准则，基于内在维度首个有效峰值自动选择层，无需训练且计算开销小。引入截断策略进一步提升检测性能，在问答和摘要基准上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="407">[407] The Compressive Knowledge Graph Hypothesis: Which Graph Facts Matter for Scientific Hypothesis Generation?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27176](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27176) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shashwat Sourav, Viktoriia Baibakova, Sanjay Das, Ran Elgedawy, Maria Mahbub, Emily Herron, Tirthankar Ghosal
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Scientific Reasoning
- **Summary**: 知识图谱在科学假设生成中哪些事实起作用尚不清楚。通过扰动电池材料KG发现，KG效用具有模型依赖性，紧凑子图可以近似全图性能，随机或拓扑子集也能恢复大部分信号，支持冗余感知的压缩知识图谱假设。

<h3 id="408">[408] SIA: Self Improving AI with Harness & Weight Updates</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27276](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27276) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Prannay Hebbar, Yogendra Manawat, Samuel Verboomen, Alesia Ivanova, Selvam Palanimalai, Kunal Bhatia, Vignesh Baskaran
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Self-Improvement
- **Summary**: AI改进依赖人工，现有方法分离了工具框架更新与权重更新。提出SIA自改进循环，元代理同时更新任务代理的工具框架和权重。在法律分类、GPU内核优化和单细胞RNA去噪任务上，组合方法优于单独框架迭代，分别提升25.1%、12.4%和20.4%。

<h3 id="409">[409] Soro: A Lightweight Foundation Model and Chatbot for Tajik</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27379](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27379) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Stanislav Liashkov, Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde, Azizjon Azimi, Khushbakht Shoymardonov, Shuhratjon Khalilbekov, Bonu Boboeva
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Low-Resource NLP, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 塔吉克语在LLM中资源匮乏，缺乏面向实际部署的模型。提出Soro模型，基于Gemma 3进行塔吉克语持续预训练和指令微调，并构建塔吉克语基准套件。模型在塔吉克语任务上显著优于同规模Gemma 3，且量化后仍保持性能，支持边缘部署。

<h3 id="410">[410] You Are in Control of Your State: Why Human Outcomes Are Controllable Through Causal State Intervention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27580](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27580) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Suraj Biswas, Saurav Gupta, Pritam Mukherjee
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; q-bio.NC
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, AI Personalization
- **Summary**: 行为科学中个体内变异性难以解释，人类结果是否可控？提出动态状态框架，将状态定义为时间索引的权重向量，决策与结果之间存在因果关系。基于24个月20万用户数据推导出可检验预测和操作要求，对数字健康、AI个性化有启示。

<h3 id="411">[411] FundaPod: A Multi-Persona Agent Pod Platform with Knowledge Graph Memory for AI-Assisted Fundamental Investment Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27864](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27864) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Di Zhu, Zheng, Zihan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Financial AI, Multi-Agent System, Knowledge Graph
- **Summary**: 现有金融LLM侧重交易信号，缺乏对机构基本面研究的支持。提出FundaPod多角色代理平台，通过知识图谱记忆系统实现独立研究和事后裁决。设计了角色蒸馏、声明性技能注册等架构机制，并通过案例研究展示。

<h3 id="412">[412] AsyncTool: Evaluating the Asynchronous Function Calling Capability under Multi-Task Scenarios</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27995](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27995) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kou Shi, Ziao Zhang, Shiting Huang, Avery Nie, Zhen Fang, Qiuchen Wang, Lin Chen, Huaian Chen, Zehui Chen, Feng Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/StoKou/repo-asynctool)
- **Summary**: 现有工具使用评估忽视异步调用中的延迟和多任务场景。提出AsyncTool基准，模拟多任务环境下带延迟的工具反馈。实验表明延迟导致性能显著下降，擅长任务切换的模型表现更好，并识别关键失败模式。

<h3 id="413">[413] The Importance of Being Statistically Earnest: A Critical Re-evaluation of GSM-Symbolic</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28700](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28700) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dominika Agnieszka Długosz, Arlindo Oliveira, Natalia Díaz-Rodríguez
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Benchmark, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: GSM-Symbolic声称LLM缺乏推理能力，但统计基础薄弱。使用广义线性混合模型重新评估20个模型，仅一半有显著性能变化，且数据集中存在较大的整数分布偏移。控制该效应后，更多模型不再显著，指出笼统结论具有误导性。

<h3 id="414">[414] Learning A Simulation-based Visual Policy for Real-world Peg In Unseen Holes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2205.04297](https://arxiv.org/abs/2205.04297) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Liang Xie, Hongxiang Yu, Kechun Xu, Tong Yang, Minhang Wang, Haojian Lu, Rong Xiong, Yue Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Sim-to-Real
- **Summary**: 机器人插销入孔任务中，现有方法难以适应未见形状且仿真到现实迁移成本高。提出解耦感知和策略的框架：快速适应的分割网络和通用的虚拟传感器网络与控制网络。仅需一分钟人工教学和数百自动标注样本，在电动汽车充电系统上实现10/10成功率，耗时2-3秒。

<h3 id="415">[415] MATNet: Multi-Level Fusion Transformer-Based Model for Day-Ahead PV Generation Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2306.10356](https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.10356) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Matteo Tortora, Francesco Conte, Gianluca Natrella, Paolo Soda
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Transformer Architecture, Energy Management
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/arco-group/MATNet)
- **Summary**: 光伏发电预测中AI模型忽略物理先验知识。提出MATNet，基于Transformer的多模态架构，采用多层次联合融合和软注意力机制。在Ausgrid数据集上RMSE为0.0445，相比最优基线提升65%，并展示了良好的零样本泛化能力和对缺失数据的鲁棒性。

<h3 id="416">[416] A Survey on Recent Advances in Conversational Data Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2405.13003](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13003) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Heydar Soudani, Roxana Petcu, Evangelos Kanoulas, Faegheh Hasibi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Survey, Dialogue System, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 对话系统训练缺乏专用数据，众包成本高且规模有限。本文系统综述多轮对话数据生成方法，涵盖开放域、任务导向和知识寻求三类系统，提出通用框架，并讨论评估指标和未来方向。

<h3 id="417">[417] Crafting Desirable Climate Trajectories with RL Explored Socio-Environmental Simulations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.07287](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07287) (replaced)
- **Authors**: James Rudd-Jones, Fiona Thendean, María Pérez-Ortiz
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Multi-Agent System, Climate Science
- **Summary**: 该论文针对传统综合评估模型在气候决策中难以处理不确定性和社会互动的不足，提出使用多智能体强化学习框架来模拟利益相关者之间的复杂互动。研究发现，合作型智能体能够持续规划出减少碳排放和改善经济的理想未来路径，而竞争性奖励函数则阻碍了理想气候目标的达成。通过状态可视化揭示了算法失败的不确定性行为，为未来政策制定提供了技术参考。

<h3 id="418">[418] Are LLMs Socially Adaptive? Contrasting Belief Evolution in Large Language Models and Humans</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.10398](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10398) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yu Lei, Hao Liu, Chengxing Xie, Songjia Liu, Zhiyu Yin, Canyu Chen, Guohao Li, Philip Torr, Zhen Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Evaluation, Social Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有LLM价值对齐基准多采用静态评估、无法捕捉决策动态性的问题，提出了基于连续经济博弈的心理模拟基准FairMindSim和信念-奖励对齐行为演化模型BREM。通过大规模人类与LLM对比实验发现，中低能力模型表现出僵化的算法攻击性（过度惩罚），而前沿模型随推理能力提升趋向于类似人类的宽大行为。BREM模型揭示了智能体在长期决策中信念与行动的一致性动态，为可解释的AI对齐分析提供了标准化协议。

<h3 id="419">[419] Jailbreaking and Mitigation of Vulnerabilities in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.15236](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15236) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Benji Peng, Hanxuan Chen, Keyu Chen, Qian Niu, Ziqian Bi, Ming Liu, Pohsun Feng, Tianyang Wang, Lawrence K.Q. Yan, Yizhu Wen, Yichao Zhang, Caitlyn Heqi Yin, Xinyuan Song, Riyang Bao, Jiacheng Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 该论文全面综述了大语言模型在提示注入和越狱攻击方面的脆弱性及防御策略。将攻击方法分类为基于提示、模型、多模态和多语言等类型，并分析了提示过滤、对齐技术、多智能体防御等防御机制的优势与不足。同时讨论了安全评估指标和基准中的挑战，指出了未来在弹性对齐策略、自动化越狱检测等方面的研究方向。

<h3 id="420">[420] Dataset-Driven Channel Masks in Transformers for Multivariate Time Series</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.23222](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23222) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Seunghan Lee, Taeyoung Park, Kibok Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Transformer Architecture, Attention Mechanism
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YonseiML/pcd)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对多变量时间序列建模中通道依赖关系常忽略数据集特性的问题，提出了部分通道依赖的概念，并通过引入通道掩码（由相似性矩阵和可学习领域参数组成）与注意力矩阵进行逐元素相乘来实现。方法增强了对数据集特定信息的利用，在多种任务和骨干网络上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="421">[421] Agent4Edu: Generating Learner Response Data by Generative Agents for Intelligent Education Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.10332](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10332) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Weibo Gao, Qi Liu, Linan Yue, Fangzhou Yao, Rui Lv, Zheng Zhang, Hao Wang, Zhenya Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Education Technology, Data Synthesis
- **Venue**: AAAI2025
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/bigdata-ustc/Agent4Edu)
- **Summary**: 该论文旨在解决个性化学习系统中离线指标与在线表现不一致的问题，提出了Agent4Edu仿真平台，利用基于LLM的生成式智能体模拟学习者行为。每个智能体包含学习者档案、记忆和动作模块，能够与自适应测试等算法交互，通过综合评估揭示了智能体与真实学习者在响应上的一致性与差异。

<h3 id="422">[422] A Composable Multimodal Framework for cine CMR-Text-Driven Prediction of Heart Failure Outcomes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.16548](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16548) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jianzhou Chen, Jinyang Sun, Xiumei Wang, Xi Chen, Heyu Chu, Guo Song, Yuji Luo, Xingping Zhou, Rong Gu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multimodal Learning, Clinical Decision Support
- **Summary**: 该论文针对心力衰竭预后预测中多源异构数据（影像、临床指标、文本记录）整合不足的问题，提出了一种可组合的多模态框架，融合心脏磁共振序列和结构化/非结构化临床数据。在多模态融合下，预后预测准确率显著优于单模态模型，并能详细评估病理指标对预后的影响，支持个性化治疗规划。

<h3 id="423">[423] Weakly Supervised Detection and Temporal Localization of Whale Calls in Long-Duration Bioacoustic Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.20838](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20838) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ragib Amin Nihal, Benjamin Yen, Runwu Shi, Takeshi Ashizawa, Kazuhiro Nakadai
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Weak Supervision, Bioacoustics, Audio Processing
- **Venue**: EUSIPCO 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Ragib-Amin-Nihal/DSMIL-Loc)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对长时被动声学监测中鲸类叫声检测需要精确时间标注的问题，提出了DSMIL-LocNet弱监督框架，仅使用录音级别的存在/缺失标签即可实现分类和时序定位。通过双流频谱-时间特征架构，在300-1800秒录音上达到0.88-0.91的F1分数，显著优于全监督基线，且无需帧级标注即可提供时间定位。

<h3 id="424">[424] Survey of End-to-End Multi-Speaker Automatic Speech Recognition for Monaural Audio</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.10975](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10975) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinlu He, Jacob Whitehill
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SD; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speech Recognition, Survey, Audio Processing
- **Venue**: Computer Speech & Language (CSL)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对单声道多说话人自动语音识别领域缺乏全面综述的问题，系统分类了端到端神经网络方法，包括单输入多输出与单输入单输出范式、近期算法改进及长语音扩展。通过基准评估比较了不同方法，并讨论了开放挑战和未来方向，为该领域提供了结构化的研究参考。

<h3 id="425">[425] Is Your LLM Overcharging You? Tokenization, Transparency, and Incentives</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.21627](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21627) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ander Artola Velasco, Stratis Tsirtsis, Nastaran Okati, Manuel Gomez-Rodriguez
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Tokenization, AI Economics
- **Summary**: 该论文揭示了付费LLM服务中按token计费机制导致提供商有动机虚报token数量，且用户无法察觉。理论分析表明，强制透明化生成过程可限制虚报，但设计了一种高效启发式算法仍能显著多收费。提出按字符数线性定价的激励相容机制，在保持平均利润率的同时消除战略虚报动机。

<h3 id="426">[426] EPiC: Efficient Video Camera Control Learning with Precise Anchor-Video Guidance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.21876](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21876) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zun Wang, Jaemin Cho, Jialu Li, Han Lin, Jaehong Yoon, Yue Zhang, Mohit Bansal
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Model, Image-to-Video
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对视频生成中锚定视频引导摄像机控制方法受点云和轨迹估计误差影响且训练成本高的问题，提出了EPiC框架。通过基于首帧可见性的掩码构建高精度锚定视频，无需相机/点云估计，并引入轻量级Anchor-ControlNet模块。在RealEstate10K和MiraData上达到最佳性能，且零样本泛化到视频到视频场景。

<h3 id="427">[427] VRAG: Learning World Models for Interactive Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.21996](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21996) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Video Generation, RAG
- **Venue**: NeurIPS 2025
- **Summary**: 该论文针对自回归视频生成中累积误差和记忆机制不足导致世界模型不一致的问题，提出视频检索增强生成框架，通过显式全局状态条件化显著减少长期误差并提升时空一致性。研究发现简单扩展上下文或传统检索增强在视频生成中效果有限，为构建可交互的世界模型提供了基准和方向。

<h3 id="428">[428] Online Fair Division with Additional Information</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.24503](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24503) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tzeh Yuan Neoh, Jannik Peters, Nicholas Teh
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, Mechanism Design, Algorithm Design
- **Summary**: 该论文针对在线环境下不可分割物品的公平分配问题，研究了利用未来信息（如归一化总分和频率预测）能带来的保证。在仅有归一化信息时，实现了比已知更强的公平性保证；在频率预测下，将离线共享类保证提升到在线设置，并提出了对噪声鲁棒的学习增强模型，匹配了最佳离线界限。

<h3 id="429">[429] Position: Text Embeddings Should Capture Implicit Semantics, Not Just Surface Meaning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.08354](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08354) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yiqun Sun, Qiang Huang, Anthony K. H. Tung, Jun Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Text Embedding, Semantic Similarity, Linguistic Resource
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/dukesun99/Implicit-Embeddings)
- **Summary**: 该论文指出当前文本嵌入研究过度关注表层语义，忽视了隐含语义（语用、意图、社会文化背景）。通过初步实验表明，最先进的嵌入模型在隐含语义任务上仅比词法基线略有提升。呼吁转向以语言学为根基的多样化训练数据和深度语义评测基准，将隐含意义作为核心建模目标。

<h3 id="430">[430] Model Fusion via Retrofitting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.00037](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00037) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Phoomraphee Luenam, Andreas Spanopoulos, Amit Sant, Thomas Hofmann, Sotiris Anagnostidis, Sidak Pal Singh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Fusion, Representation Learning, Transfer Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/AndrewSpano/model-fusion-via-retrofitting)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对模型融合中因置换不变性、随机初始化和异构数据导致的表示差异问题，提出了一种以神经元为中心的融合算法。通过将中间神经元分组为目标表示，并利用神经元归因分数突出重要特征，训练融合模型的子网络进行逼近。在VGG、ResNet和ViT上的实验表明，该方法在零样本和非独立同分布场景下显著优于现有融合方法。

<h3 id="431">[431] Taming Data Challenges in ML-based Security Tasks Using Generative AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.06092](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06092) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shravya Kanchi, Neal Mangaokar, Aravind Cheruvu, Sifat Muhammad Abdullah, Shirin Nilizadeh, Atul Prakash, Bimal Viswanath
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Data Augmentation, Generative Model
- **Venue**: 2026 ACM Asia Conference on Computer and Communications Security (AsiaCCS 2026)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对机器学习安全分类器面临的数据挑战（如样本不足、标签噪声），提出使用生成式AI合成数据增强训练集。在7个安全任务上使用6种GenAI方法，发现可显著提升性能，在约180个训练样本时改进高达32.6%，并能快速适应概念漂移。同时指出某些GenAI方法在初始化上存在困难，以及任务特性影响增益。

<h3 id="432">[432] MENTOR: Efficient Multimodal-Conditioned Tuning for Autoregressive Vision Generation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.09574](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.09574) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Autoregressive Model, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HaozheZhao/MENTOR)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对文本到图像模型在精细视觉控制和多模态输入平衡方面的不足，提出了MENTOR高效多模态条件微调框架。采用两阶段训练（多模态对齐和指令微调），实现多模态输入与图像输出的令牌级对齐，无需辅助适配器。在DreamBench++上表现优异，在概念保持和提示遵循上超越基线，且训练效率优于扩散模型。

<h3 id="433">[433] Finding DoRI: Discovery of Retained Images in Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.16880](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.16880) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Antoni Kowalczuk, Dominik Hintersdorf, Lukas Struppek, Kristian Kersting, Adam Dziedzic, Franziska Boenisch

- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该文质疑文本到图像扩散模型中记忆现象可局部化的假设，通过实验证明记忆触发分布在文本嵌入空间全局，且不同修剪方法识别的不一致，提出对抗微调作为更鲁棒的缓解方法。结果发现即使修剪权重，微小扰动仍可触发复制，对抗微调能有效缓解。

<h3 id="434">[434] Page image classification for content-specific data processing</h3>

<h3 id="435">[435] Approximate Proportionality in Online Fair Division</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.03253](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03253) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Davin Choo, Winston Fu, Derek Khu, Tzeh Yuan Neoh, Tze-Yang Poon, Nicholas Teh
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Online Fair Division
- **Summary**: 在线公平分配问题中，研究PROP1可近似性。证明三种贪心规则无法对抗自适应对手；在非自适应对手下，均匀随机分配达到有意义近似；给定最大物品值预测，设计鲁棒算法并证明EF1等仍不可近似。

<h3 id="438">[438] Less Is More: Elevating RAG via Performance-Driven Context Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.19282](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.19282) (replaced)

<h3 id="439">[439] GRPO is Secretly a Process Reward Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.21154](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21154) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Michael Sullivan, Alexander Koller
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Reward Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 理论证明GRPO算法实际上隐式等价于过程奖励模型，并指出GRPO目标在步长和奖励不平衡时的缺陷。提出λ-GRPO修正，在推理任务上比标准GRPO更快达到更优性能，且几乎不增加训练成本。

<h3 id="440">[440] Towards Foundation Models for Zero-Shot Time Series Anomaly Detection: Leveraging Synthetic Data and Relative Context Discrepancy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.21190](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21190) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tian Lan, Hao Duong Le, Jinbo Li, Wenjun He, Meng Wang, Chenghao Liu, Chen Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Time Series Forecasting, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 针对时间序列异常检测模型零样本泛化难，提出TimeRCD，基于相对上下文差异的新预训练范式，使用合成数据训练。在零样本TSAD上显著优于现有通用及专用基础模型。

<h3 id="441">[441] Benchmarking LLM-Assisted Blue Teaming via Standardized Threat Hunting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.23571](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23571) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuqiao Meng, Luoxi Tang, Feiyang Yu, Xi Li, Guanhua Yan, Ping Yang, Zhaohan Xi

- **Summary**: 为系统评估LLM在蓝队威胁狩猎中的能力，提出CyberTeam基准，标准化工作流为30个任务和9个操作模块。评估表明标准化设计提升LLM表现，开放推理在真实威胁狩猎中存在局限。

<h3 id="442">[442] Uncovering Vulnerabilities of LLM-Assisted Cyber Threat Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.23573](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23573) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuqiao Meng, Luoxi Tang, Feiyang Yu, Jinyuan Jia, Guanhua Yan, Ping Yang, Zhaohan Xi
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Cybersecurity, Threat Detection
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在CTI推理中的可靠性差距，通过人工分类识别三种领域特定认知失败：虚假相关、矛盾知识和泛化受限。因果干预验证后，针对性防御显著降低失败率。

<h3 id="443">[443] AtomWorld: A Benchmark for Evaluating Spatial Reasoning in Large Language Models on Crystalline Materials</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.04704](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04704) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Taoyuze Lv, Alexander Chen, Fengyu Xie, Chu Wu, Jeffrey Meng, Dongzhan Zhou, Yingheng Wang, Bram Hoex, Zhicheng Zhong, Tong Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL

- **Summary**: 提出AtomWorld基准，评估LLM在晶体材料结构修改任务中的空间推理能力。包含10种动作和4种建模类别，Claude Opus 4.6最佳，但复杂操作成功率低（如旋转低于12%）。

<h3 id="444">[444] The Impact of Semantic Pairs on Self-Supervised Representation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.08722](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08722) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 通过控制实验研究语义正对（同类别不同实例）在自监督表示学习中的作用。在ImageNet-1K上对比语义正对与增强正对，发现语义正对在迁移学习和目标检测上持续提升泛化，对比学习受益最大。

<h3 id="445">[445] Obfuscation Rules for Detecting and Detoxifying Korean Toxicity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.10961](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10961) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yejin Lee, Su-Hyeon Kim, Hyundong Jin, Dayoung Kim, Yeonsoo Kim, Yo-Sub Han

- **Summary**: 针对韩语毒性表达常通过形态和正字法变体混淆而未被研究，构建KOTOX数据集，定义混淆规则并生成配对句子。模型训练后在混淆文本上性能提升，且不牺牲非混淆文本性能。

<h3 id="446">[446] Offline Reinforcement Learning with Generative Trajectory Policies</h3>

<h3 id="447">[447] Beyond Accuracy: Are Time Series Foundation Models Well-Calibrated?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.16060](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16060) (replaced)

<h3 id="448">[448] Empathic Prompting: Non-Verbal Context Integration for Multimodal LLM Conversations</h3>

<h3 id="449">[449] LoCoT2V-Bench: Benchmarking Long-Form and Complex Text-to-Video Generation</h3>

<h3 id="450">[450] ScheduleStream: Temporal Planning with Samplers for GPU-Accelerated Multi-Arm Task and Motion Planning & Scheduling</h3>

<h3 id="451">[451] An accuracy-aware extension to LRP-based pruning for CNNs to prevent cascading accuracy degradation in data-scarce transfer learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.10861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.10861) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Transfer Learning
- **Venue**: scientific reports
- **Summary**: 针对数据稀缺微调中预训练CNN冗余导致效率低且现有LRP剪枝方法引起级联精度下降的问题，提出了精度感知的剪枝控制机制。在VGG16上使AUC提高约15%，有效抑制精度下降。

<h3 id="452">[452] Enhancing Reinforcement Learning in 3D Environments through Semantic Segmentation: A Case Study in ViZDoom</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.11703](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11703) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO

<h3 id="453">[453] MiAD: Mirage Atom Diffusion for De Novo Crystal Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.14426](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14426) (replaced)

<h3 id="454">[454] ReflexGrad: Within-Episode Failure Recovery in LLM Agents via Progress-Gated Dual-Process Routing</h3>

<h3 id="455">[455] Evaluating Dataset Watermarking for Fine-tuning Traceability of Customized Diffusion Models: A Comprehensive Benchmark and Removal Approach</h3>

<h3 id="456">[456] BioArc: Discovering Optimal Neural Architectures for Biological Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.00283](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00283) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yi Fang, Haoran Xu, Jiaxin Han, Sirui Ding, Yizhi Wang, Yue Wang, Xuan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.QM

<h3 id="457">[457] Topological Order in Neural Wavefunctions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.01863](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01863) (replaced)

<h3 id="458">[458] E-valuator: Reliable Agent Verifiers with Sequential Hypothesis Testing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.03109](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03109) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 针对智能体验证器分数缺乏正确性保证的问题，提出e-valuator方法，将黑盒验证器分数转化为具有可控虚警率的决策规则。在六个数据集上提供更好的统计功效和虚警率控制。

<h3 id="459">[459] E3AD: An Emotion-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model for Human-Centric End-to-End Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.04733](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04733) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yihong Tang, Haicheng Liao, Tong Nie, Junlin He, Ao Qu, Kehua Chen, Wei Ma, Zhenning Li, Lijun Sun, Chengzhong Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Emotion Recognition, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对端到端自动驾驶忽略乘客情绪的问题，提出E3AD情感感知VLA框架。在真实数据集上改进视觉基础和轨迹规划，在情绪估计上达到SOTA VAD相关性。

<h3 id="460">[460] The Best of the Two Worlds: Harmonizing Semantic and Hash IDs for Sequential Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.10388](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10388) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Sequential Recommendation
- **Summary**: 针对序列推荐中哈希ID在长尾场景脆弱、语义ID存在协作淹没的问题，提出H2Rec框架融合HID和SID。在三个公开基准和商业平台上实现头部和尾部推荐质量的更好平衡。

<h3 id="461">[461] A Review of Learning-Based Motion Planning: Toward a Data-Driven Optimal Control Approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.11944](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11944) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jia Hu, Yang Chang, Haoran Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Motion Planning, Survey
- **Summary**: 针对自动驾驶运动规划中传统规则与学习方法之间权衡的问题，提出数据驱动最优控制（DDOC）范式综述。构建了DDOC框架的路线图，并识别四个未来研究方向。

<h3 id="462">[462] Revisiting the Reliability of Language Models in Instruction-Following</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.14754](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14754) (replaced)

<h3 id="463">[463] HD-Prot: A Protein Language Model for Joint Sequence-Structure Modeling with Continuous Structure Tokens</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.15133](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15133) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yi Zhou, Haohao Qu, Yunqing Liu, Shanru Lin, Le Song, Wenqi Fan
- **Subjects**: cs.CE; cs.AI
